def drain_redacted(read_chunk,write,token,limit=128*1024**2,chunk_size=65536):
 # Retain only a possible cross-boundary token prefix; never allocate a line.
 require(isinstance(token,bytes) and 16<=len(token)<=4096,'evidence','redaction token bound')
 carry=b'';received=written=0
 def emit(data):
  nonlocal written
  written+=len(data);require(written<limit,'evidence','log output bound')
  if data:write(data)
 while True:
  chunk=read_chunk(chunk_size)
  require(isinstance(chunk,bytes) and len(chunk)<=chunk_size,'evidence','log read bound')
  if not chunk:break
  received+=len(chunk);require(received<limit,'evidence','log input bound')
  data=carry+chunk;stop=max(0,len(data)-len(token)+1);position=0
  while position<stop:
   found=data.find(token,position)
   if found<0 or found>=stop:emit(data[position:stop]);position=stop;break
   emit(data[position:found]);emit(b'[REDACTED]');position=found+len(token)
  carry=data[position:]
  require(len(carry)<len(token),'evidence','redaction carry bound')

 return {'input_bytes':received,'output_bytes':written}
def seam_transition(old,r):

  if self.barrier and self.barrier.active:self.check_barrier()
  if self.observer and not noproc:self.observer.register_descendants()
 @io_guard

 def journal(self,kind,**data):
  with (OUT/'journal.jsonl').open('a') as f:f.write(json.dumps(dict(kind=kind,monotonic=time.monotonic(),utc=time.time(),**data))+'\n')

 def spawn(self,name,args,env,cpus):
  require(not self.launches_closed and self.barrier is not None and self.barrier.active,'launch','launch lock/barrier not active');self.barrier.verify(0o700)
  self.gates();require(sha(Path(args[0]))==self.admission['executable_pins'][str(Path(args[0]).resolve())]['sha256'],'launch','spawn executable differs');plan=ROOT/'admission'/(name+'-prelaunch.json');save(plan,dict(argv=args,uid=1001,cpus=sorted(cpus),overall_deadline=self.end,supervisor_sha256=sha(Path(__file__)),admission=self.admission,immutable_manifest=self.import_manifest,scope=self.result['scope'],env={k:v for k,v in env.items() if k!='SUB01_ADMIN_TOKEN'}),mode=0o400)
  persistent_plan=PERSIST/(name+'-prelaunch.json');shutil.copyfile(plan,persistent_plan);require(sha(plan)==sha(persistent_plan),'evidence','prelaunch plan readback')
  def drop():no_new_privileges();os.setgroups([]);os.setgid(1001);os.setuid(1001);os.sched_setaffinity(0,cpus)
  if name=='native':
   self.result['sdk_prelaunch']=sdk_prelaunch(env);save(OUT/'sdk-prelaunch.json',self.result['sdk_prelaunch'])
   self.result['ddc_prelaunch']=ddc.prelaunch(args,env,cache_probe=self.result['ddc_access']);save(OUT/'ddc-prelaunch.json',self.result['ddc_prelaunch'])
  child=subprocess.Popen(args,env=env,cwd='/home/unreal',stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,start_new_session=True,preexec_fn=drop,close_fds=True)
  entry=None
  try:
   entry=identity(child.pid);require(entry and entry['uid']==1001 and entry['sid']==child.pid,'launch','child identity')
   self.children.append((child,entry));self.observer.record_launch(child.pid,entry['ticks'],name);self.observer.register_owned(child.pid,entry['ticks'],name);self.journal('launch',name=name,identity=entry)
   def drain():
    log=None
    try:
     with IO_LOCK:log=(OUT/(name+'.log')).open('xb')
     def write_chunk(data):log.write(data);log.flush()
     def read_chunk(size):
      data=os.read(child.stdout.fileno(),size)
      if not data:detail['eof']=True;detail['eof_monotonic_ns']=time.monotonic_ns()
      return data
     drain_redacted(read_chunk,write_chunk,self.token.encode())
    except BaseException:self.errors.append(traceback.format_exc());self.stop.set()
    finally:
     with IO_LOCK:
      if log:log.close()
      child.stdout.close()
   t=threading.Thread(target=drain,daemon=True,name='drain-'+name);detail={'name':t.name,'launch_identity':entry,'pipe_inode':os.fstat(child.stdout.fileno()).st_ino,'eof':False};self.worker_details.append(detail);t.start();self.threads.append(t);return child,entry
  except BaseException:
   # Child is not yet reaped: successful Popen guarantees owned new session.
   if entry is None:
    self.children.append((child,{'pid':child.pid,'ticks':None,'unverified_role':name}))
    self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'lifecycle','error':'Initial Popen identity unavailable; no signal/adoption allowed','pid':child.pid})
   # Registered launch cleanup is performed by finish's exact lifecycle pass.
   raise
 def collect_lifecycle_failures(self):
  if not self.observer:return
  for failure in self.observer.lifecycle_failures:
   key=json.dumps(failure,sort_keys=True,default=str)
   if key not in self.lifecycle_failure_seen:
    self.lifecycle_failure_seen.add(key);self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'lifecycle','error':failure})
 def quiesce_controls(self):
  # stop is set before joining; drain threads deliberately remain active.
  self.stop.set();deadline=time.monotonic()+2
  for thread in self.control_threads:thread.join(max(0,deadline-time.monotonic()))
  self.result['control_workers_before_teardown']=[{'name':thread.name,'alive':thread.is_alive()} for thread in self.control_threads]
  self.control_quiesced=not any(thread.is_alive() for thread in self.control_threads)
  require(self.control_quiesced,'cleanup','fixture/sampler did not quiesce before teardown')
 def settle_owned(self):
  # One budget and escalation clock; no phase/discovery failure restarts either.
  started=time.monotonic();deadline=started+7.5;native_deadline=started+5
  self.result['teardown_budget']={'started_monotonic':started,'deadline_monotonic':deadline,'native_deadline_monotonic':native_deadline}
  phase='native';failure_only=False;last=None;chronology=[];completed_history=set();fallback_bindings={};self.result['teardown_chronology']=chronology
  def note(event,**data):chronology.append(dict(event=event,monotonic=time.monotonic(),deadline=deadline,**data))
  def fail_order(reason):
   nonlocal failure_only,phase
   if not failure_only:
    self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'ordered-teardown','error':reason})
    note('failure-only-enter',reason=reason);failure_only=True;phase='failure-only'
  note('phase',phase=phase)
  while time.monotonic()<deadline:
   discovery_failed=False
   if self.observer:
    try:self.observer.register_descendants()
    except BaseException as exc:
     discovery_failed=True;key='discovery:'+repr(exc)
     if key not in self.lifecycle_failure_seen:
      self.lifecycle_failure_seen.add(key);self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'lifecycle/discovery','error':repr(exc)})
   if discovery_failed:fail_order('Discovery failed; ordered prerequisites unproven')
   records=dict(getattr(self.observer,'ancestry',{}));records.update({p:r for p,r in getattr(self.observer,'owned',{}).items() if p!=os.getpid()})
   warm=getattr(self.observer,'warm',None)
   def role(pid,record):
    if warm is not None and pid==warm.pid:return 'reader'
    return 'xvfb' if record.get('role')=='xvfb' else 'native'
   if phase in ('reader','xvfb'):
    prerequisite={p for p,r in records.items() if role(p,r)=='native' or (phase=='xvfb' and role(p,r)=='reader')}
    if not prerequisite<=completed_history:fail_order('New or unresolved prerequisite after phase transition')
   selected=set(records) if failure_only else {p for p,r in records.items() if role(p,r)==phase}
   # An unregistered successful Popen is cleanup-only and makes the run fail.
   unregistered=[(child,entry) for child,entry in self.children if child.pid not in records and child.returncode is None]
   if warm is not None and warm.pid not in records and warm.returncode is None:unregistered.append((warm,None))
   if unregistered:fail_order('Direct Popen lacks registered initial identity/admission');selected=set(records)
   signal_decision=time.monotonic();sig=signal.SIGTERM if signal_decision-started<2 else signal.SIGKILL
   if self.observer:
    try:last=self.observer.lifecycle_cleanup_pass(sig,pids=selected,deadline=deadline,escalation_started=started)
    except BaseException as exc:last={'pending':['cleanup pass failed'],'errors':[repr(exc)],'outcomes':[]}
    self.collect_lifecycle_failures()
   else:last={'pending':[],'errors':[],'outcomes':[]}
   note('pass',phase=phase,signal=int(sig),signal_decision_monotonic=signal_decision,selected=sorted(selected),result=last)
   for outcome in last['outcomes']:
    if outcome.get('state')=='REAPED':
     for child,entry in self.children:
      if child.pid==outcome['pid'] and entry['ticks']==outcome['identity']['ticks']:child.returncode=outcome['returncode']
     if warm is not None and warm.pid==outcome['pid'] and records[warm.pid]['identity']['ticks']==outcome['identity']['ticks']:warm.returncode=outcome['returncode']
   for child,entry in unregistered:
    try:
     # Exact Popen handle only; this branch grants no live admission. Every
     # signal repeats the known identity checks; a foreign wait cannot pass.
     require(time.monotonic()<deadline,'cleanup','Original cleanup deadline before direct fallback')
     current=identity(child.pid)
     require(current is not None,'cleanup','Unregistered child gone/identity unreadable before exact wait')
     expected=fallback_bindings.get(child.pid,entry)
     require(expected is not None and expected.get('ticks') is not None,'cleanup','Unregistered Popen has no initial start-ticks proof; cannot adopt current PID')
     fallback_bindings[child.pid]=expected
     require(all(current[k]==expected[k] for k in ('pid','ticks','uid','sid')),'cleanup','unregistered identity drift')
     require(identity(child.pid)==current,'cleanup','unregistered repeated identity mismatch')
     if current['state']!='Z':
      actual_sig=signal.SIGTERM if time.monotonic()-started<2 else signal.SIGKILL
      note('unregistered-signal',pid=child.pid,identity=current,signal=int(actual_sig));os.kill(child.pid,actual_sig)
     waited,status=os.waitpid(child.pid,os.WNOHANG)
     note('unregistered-wait',pid=child.pid,identity=current,waited=waited,wait_status=status)
     if waited==0:
      require(status==0,'cleanup','Invalid pending direct wait');last['pending'].append(child.pid);continue
     require(waited==child.pid and identity(child.pid) is None,'cleanup','unregistered direct wait/disappearance incomplete')
     child.returncode=os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status)
     self.result.setdefault('unregistered_direct_waits',[]).append({'pid':child.pid,'identity':current,'returncode':child.returncode,'live_admitted':False})
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:last['pending'].append(child.pid)
    except BaseException as exc:last['errors'].append(repr(exc));last['pending'].append(child.pid)
   completed={o['pid'] for o in last['outcomes'] if o.get('state')=='REAPED' and o['pid'] in records and o['identity']['ticks']==records[o['pid']]['identity']['ticks']}
   completed_history.update(completed)
   for pid in selected-completed:
    if pid not in last['pending']:last['pending'].append(pid)
   if last['errors']:fail_order('Native/client or dependency identity/wait prerequisite failed')
   if phase=='native' and time.monotonic()>=native_deadline:
    fail_order('Native/client five-second allocation exhausted');continue
   if not last['pending'] and not last['errors']:
    if phase=='native':phase='reader';note('phase',phase=phase);continue
    if phase=='reader':phase='xvfb';note('phase',phase=phase);continue
    break
   remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
   if remaining>0:time.sleep(min(.05,remaining))
  self.result['lifecycle_cleanup']=last
  self.result['teardown_mode']='failure-only' if failure_only else 'ordered'
  if last is None or last['pending'] or last['errors'] or time.monotonic()>=deadline:
   self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'unresolved-wait','error':'Original 7.5-second cleanup deadline or unresolved identity/wait','last':last})
   raise Failure('cleanup','known children or terminal outcomes unresolved at original deadline')
  require(all(child.returncode is not None and identity(child.pid) is None for child,entry in self.children),'cleanup','direct child wait/disappearance incomplete')
  if warm is not None:require(warm.returncode is not None and identity(warm.pid) is None,'cleanup','warm reader exact wait/disappearance incomplete')
  # Drains were never joined/stopped before all producer waits; join only in
  # the remainder of the same budget, preserving actual EOF proof.
  for thread in self.threads:thread.join(max(0,deadline-time.monotonic()))
  self.result['workers']=[{'name':thread.name,'alive':thread.is_alive()} for thread in self.threads]
  self.result['drain_pipes']=self.worker_details
  require(time.monotonic()<deadline and not any(thread.is_alive() for thread in self.threads),'cleanup','workers/pipe holders unresolved; no final census or restoration')
  require(all(r.get('eof') is True for r in self.worker_details),'cleanup','producer pipe EOF unproven')
  self.cleanup_proven=True;note('settled',mode=self.result['teardown_mode'])
 def sample(self,initial_sequence=None):
  read_start=time.monotonic()
  if self.last_sample_start is not None:require(read_start-self.last_sample_start<=.25,'fixture','sample start-to-start deadline')
  self.last_sample_start=read_start
  self.gates(noproc=True);require(same(self.game[1]),'fixture','native identity lost')
  r=get(RECEIPT);now=time.time()
  require(-.05<=now-r['captured_utc']<=.5,'fixture','per-sample receipt freshness')
  require(r.get('native_pid')==self.game[1]['pid'] and r.get('native_start_ticks')==self.game[1]['ticks'],'fixture','receipt identity')
  # Only bounded startup admission may wait on the exact pre-submission receipt.
  # It never becomes an accepted/stable sample and cannot be reused after advance.
  if initial_sequence is not None and self.previous_receipt is None and self.stabilized_at is None and r.get('event_sequence')==initial_sequence:
   require(r.get('schema_version')==2,'fixture','initial receipt schema')
   self.journal('initial-fixture-not-yet-admitted',receipt=r);return None
  require(r.get('schema_version')==2 and r.get('state')=='reading' and r.get('requested_state')=='reading','state','reading v2 absent')
  require(r.get('clip')==WEBSITE and r.get('clip_fallback_frozen') is False,'state','wrong/fallback clip')
  require(r.get('camera')=='face' and r.get('face_camera_blend',0)>=.999 and r.get('camera_blend',1)<=.001,'state','camera blends')
  require(r.get('source_frame_id')=='controlled-reconstruction-fixture' and r.get('intensity')==.5 and r.get('face_camera_tuning_sequence')==1792871330177,'fixture','fixture/tuning fields differ')
  accepted=self.events.get(r.get('event_sequence'))
  require(accepted is not None and accepted['expires_utc']>=now and accepted['expires_utc']-accepted['captured_utc']==1.5,'fixture','accepted fixture absent/expired')
  if self.previous_receipt:
   old=self.previous_receipt
   require(r['captured_utc']>=old['captured_utc'],'fixture','receipt timestamp regressed')
   require(r['event_sequence']>=old['event_sequence'],'fixture','receipt sequence regressed')
   seam_transition(old,r)
  require(r['event_sequence']<=self.seq,'fixture','future/unsubmitted fixture sequence')
  if self.previous_receipt is None or r['event_sequence']>self.previous_receipt['event_sequence']:
   self.last_actual_advance=read_start;self.accepted_event_count+=1
  if self.stabilized_at is not None:require(read_start-max(self.stabilized_at,self.last_actual_advance)<=.75,'fixture','actual-event liveness exceeded .75s')
  self.previous_receipt=r;self.latest_sample={'monotonic_ns':time.monotonic_ns(),'receipt':r};self.receipt_samples.append(self.latest_sample);self.journal('receipt',receipt=r);return r
 def fixture(self):
  try:
   while not self.stop.is_set():
    self.gates(noproc=True);require(same(self.game[1]),'fixture','native lost');self.seq+=1;now=time.time()
    doc=dict(schema='sub01.private-streamer-visual-event.v2',source='streamer_agent',native_pid=self.game[1]['pid'],native_start_ticks=self.game[1]['ticks'],captured_utc=now,expires_utc=now+1.5,sequence=self.seq,state='reading',gaze_yaw_deg=0,gaze_pitch_deg=-4,nod_strength=0,intensity=.5,camera='face',source_frame_id='controlled-reconstruction-fixture')
    self.events[self.seq]=doc;save(EVENT,doc,owner=(1001,1001));self.journal('synthetic-fixture',document=doc);self.stop.wait(.5)
  except BaseException:self.errors.append(traceback.format_exc());self.stop.set()
 def execute(self):
  u=pwd.getpwnam('unreal');require(u.pw_uid==u.pw_gid==1001,'launch','runtime UID mismatch')
  allowed=sorted(os.sched_getaffinity(0));quota=Path('/sys/fs/cgroup/cpu.max').read_text().split();budget=len(allowed) if quota[0]=='max' else min(len(allowed),int(quota[0])/int(quota[1]))

 @io_guard
 def preserve_outputs(self):
  # OUT is mutable working evidence, never a sealed manifest target.
  # Each publication copies into an independent generation; old generations
  # and their manifests are never overwritten, even on late failure.
  save(OUT/'main.json',self.result)
  require(PERSIST.exists(),'evidence','persistent output missing')
  generations=PERSIST/'generations';generations.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  self.generation_number+=1;name='generation-%04d'%self.generation_number
  temporary=generations/('.incomplete-'+name+'-'+secrets.token_hex(8));temporary.mkdir(mode=0o700)
  destination=generations/name;require(not destination.exists(),'evidence','generation already exists')
  inventory={}
  def sync_directory(directory):
   fd=os.open(directory,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_DIRECTORY|os.O_NOFOLLOW)
   try:os.fsync(fd)
   finally:os.close(fd)
  def copy_file(source,relative):
   require(stat.S_ISREG(source.lstat().st_mode),'evidence','nonregular generation source')
   before=sha(source);target=temporary/relative;target.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
   with source.open('rb') as src,target.open('xb') as dst:
    shutil.copyfileobj(src,dst);dst.flush();os.fsync(dst.fileno())
   require(before==sha(source)==sha(target),'evidence','generation copy/readback mismatch')
   inventory[relative]={'bytes':target.stat().st_size,'sha256':before}
  for source in sorted(OUT.rglob('*')):
   require(not source.is_symlink(),'evidence','symlink output')
   if source.is_file() and source.name!='xauthority':copy_file(source,'files/'+str(source.relative_to(OUT)))
  for source in sorted((ROOT/'admission').glob('*-prelaunch.json')):copy_file(source,'launch-plans/'+source.name)
  if self.barrier and self.barrier.record_owned:
   for source in sorted(BARRIER_RECOVERY.glob('*.json')):copy_file(source,'root-barrier/'+source.name)
  save(temporary/'result.json',self.result)
  inventory['result.json']={'bytes':(temporary/'result.json').stat().st_size,'sha256':sha(temporary/'result.json')}
  document={'schema':'sub01.visual-preservation-generation.v1','generation':name,'previous':self.previous_generation,'files':inventory,'run_status':self.result['status'],'preservation_scope':'immutable copied evidence only; not run/visual/performance acceptance'}
  save(temporary/'manifest.json',document);manifest_hash=sha(temporary/'manifest.json')
  for relative,row in inventory.items():
   target=temporary/relative;require(target.stat().st_size==row['bytes'] and sha(target)==row['sha256'],'evidence','generation final readback')
  for directory in sorted([x for x in temporary.rglob('*') if x.is_dir()],key=lambda x:len(x.parts),reverse=True):sync_directory(directory)
  sync_directory(temporary);require(time.monotonic()<self.end,'timeout','overall deadline exceeded during preservation')
  os.rename(temporary,destination);sync_directory(generations)
  require(sha(destination/'manifest.json')==manifest_hash,'evidence','published manifest mismatch')
  pointer={'schema':'sub01.visual-preservation-latest.v1','generation':name,'manifest_sha256':manifest_hash}
  self.previous_generation=pointer # a completely published directory remains chained if pointer publication fails
  save(PERSIST/'LATEST-GENERATION.json',pointer);sync_directory(PERSIST)
  require(get(PERSIST/'LATEST-GENERATION.json')==pointer,'evidence','latest pointer readback')
  self.previous_generation=pointer
 def finish(self):
  self.launches_closed=True
  self.result['accepted_actual_event_count']=self.accepted_event_count
  self.stop.set()
  try:self.quiesce_controls()
  except BaseException:self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'control-quiescence','error':traceback.format_exc()})
  # Even failed control quiescence must not skip known-child termination/reaping.
  if self.observer:self.result['fifo_preservation_location']=str(self.observer.old) if self.observer.old else None
  try:self.settle_owned()
  except BaseException:self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'cleanup','error':traceback.format_exc()})
  if self.game and self.events:
   try:
    event=get(EVENT);require(event['native_pid']==self.game[1]['pid'] and event['native_start_ticks']==self.game[1]['ticks'],'fixture','foreign event during cleanup')
    remaining=event['expires_utc']-time.time()
    require(remaining<=1.5,'fixture','event expiry exceeded')
    if remaining>0:time.sleep(remaining)
    require(time.time()>=event['expires_utc'],'fixture','synthetic event not expired')
   except BaseException:self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'fixture/cleanup','error':traceback.format_exc()})
  if self.observer:
   try:
    require(self.cleanup_proven,'cleanup','no complete child/worker quiescence; restoration blocked')
    # Ordinary complete census is restored only after lifecycle waits and all
    # workers are settled; access-barrier audit wraps this corroboration.
    self.observer.corroborate('settled-before-fifo-restoration',())
    self.observer.restore_fifo()
    if self.result['status']=='CAPTURE_COMPLETE_READBACK_PENDING':
     self.result['readback']=self.observer.validate_saved()
     # Decode every preserved JPEG after native/reader/X teardown, never just a midpoint.
     remaining=self.end-time.monotonic();require(remaining>5,'timeout','no decode/preservation reserve')
     with (OUT/'decode.stderr').open('xb') as err:
      subprocess.run(['ffmpeg','-v','error','-xerror','-threads','2','-pattern_type','glob','-i',str(OUT/'fifo-evidence/frames/*.jpg'),'-f','null','-'],stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=err,check=True,timeout=min(20,remaining-5),close_fds=True)
     self.result['full_jpeg_decode']='PASS';self.result['status']='TECHNICAL_PASS_VISUAL_PENDING'
   except BaseException:self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'collector/cleanup','error':traceback.format_exc()})
  for t in self.threads:t.join(1)
  if self.errors:self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'fixture/worker','error':self.errors})
  if any(t.is_alive() for t in self.threads):self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'cleanup','error':'supervisor worker thread remains'})
  if time.monotonic()>self.end:self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'timeout','error':'overall 1020s exceeded'})
  if not (STREAM/'STOP').is_file():self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'prerequisite','error':'STOP lost'})
  if self.baseline is not None:
   if manifest(ORIGINAL)!=self.baseline or (self.import_manifest is not None and manifest(IMPORT_PROJECT)!=self.import_manifest) or (self.created_outputs and PROJECT.exists() and manifest(PROJECT)!=self.import_manifest) or sha(TUNING)!=TUNING_HASH:self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'preservation','error':'source/import/tuning changed'})
  if 'ddc_runtime' in self.result:
   try:
    self.result['ddc_runtime_final']=ddc.runtime_log_proof((OUT/'native.log').read_bytes().decode('utf-8'));save(OUT/'ddc-runtime-final.json',self.result['ddc_runtime_final'])
   except BaseException:self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'ddc-final-evidence','error':traceback.format_exc()})
  if self.result['failures']:self.result['status']='FAILED'
  if self.created_outputs:self.preserve_outputs()
  # Baselines and complete output readback above occur while traversal is denied.
  if self.barrier:
   if self.barrier.active:
    require(self.cleanup_proven and not any(t.is_alive() for t in self.threads) and not any(x['kind']=='preservation' for x in self.result['failures']),'preservation','unsafe restoration: leave ancestor0700')
    if self.observer:self.observer.corroborate('settled-before-barrier-restoration',())
    self.barrier.restore();self.result['access_barrier']='RESTORED_0710'
   else:self.barrier.close()
   # Append final census/restoration records; original hashes were verified active.
   if self.created_outputs:self.preserve_outputs()

 def run(self):
  print('SUB01_VISUAL_BEGIN',flush=True)
  try:self.result['status']='PREPARING';self.prepare();self.execute()
  except BaseException as e:self.result['failures'].append({'kind':getattr(e,'kind','UNEXPLAINED_FAILURE'),'error':traceback.format_exc()})
  finally:
   try:self.finish()
   except BaseException:
    self.result['status']='FAILED';self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'cleanup/evidence','error':traceback.format_exc()})
    if self.barrier:
     try:self.barrier.blocked(self.result['failures'][-1]['error'])
     except BaseException:self.result['failures'].append({'kind':'barrier/recovery','error':traceback.format_exc()})
    if self.created_outputs:
     try:self.preserve_outputs()
     except BaseException:
      self.result['preservation_incomplete']=traceback.format_exc()
      # Working OUT is never sealed. Do not rewrite any published generation.
      try:save(OUT/'main.json',self.result)
      except BaseException:pass
  print('SUB01_VISUAL_END '+json.dumps({'status':self.result['status'],'failure_kinds':[x['kind'] for x in self.result['failures']]}),flush=True)
  require(self.result['status']=='TECHNICAL_PASS_VISUAL_PENDING','evidence','technical run did not pass')

class Block(RuntimeError): pass
def need(ok,why):
    if not ok: raise Block(why)
def sha_bytes(b): return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def write_new(p,value):
    b=(json.dumps(value,sort_keys=True)+'\n').encode()
    fd=os.open(p,os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREAT|os.O_EXCL|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_CLOEXEC,0o600)
    with os.fdopen(fd,'wb') as f:f.write(b);f.flush();os.fsync(f.fileno())
def identity(pid,proc=Path('/proc')):
    p=proc/str(pid); fields=(p/'stat').read_text().rsplit(')',1)[1].split()
    rows={x.split(':',1)[0]:x.split(':',1)[1].split() for x in (p/'status').read_text().splitlines() if ':'in x}
    return {'pid':int(pid),'ticks':int(fields[19]),'ppid':int(fields[1]),'sid':int(fields[3]),'state':fields[0],
            'uids':rows['Uid'],'gids':rows['Gid'],'groups':rows['Groups'],'cap_eff':rows['CapEff'][0],
            'cap_prm':rows['CapPrm'][0],'cap_amb':rows['CapAmb'][0],'no_new_privs':int(rows['NoNewPrivs'][0]),
            'cmdline_sha256':sha_bytes((p/'cmdline').read_bytes()),'comm':(p/'comm').read_text().strip()}
def stable(a,b): return all(a[k]==b[k] for k in ('pid','ticks','uids','gids','groups','cap_eff','cap_prm','cap_amb','no_new_privs','ppid','sid'))
def require_unprivileged(i):
    need(i['no_new_privs']==1,'Native/helper no_new_privs absent')
    need(set(i['uids'])=={'1001'} and set(i['gids'])=={'1001'},'Native/helper UID/GID elevated')
    need(set(i['groups'])<={'1001'},'Native/helper supplementary groups not admitted')
    need(all(int(i[k],16)==0 for k in ('cap_eff','cap_prm','cap_amb')),'Native/helper capabilities elevated')
def path_reference(path):
    target=os.readlink(path);s=path.stat()
    need(os.readlink(path)==target,'Process path changed during census')
    again=path.stat();need((s.st_dev,s.st_ino,s.st_mode)==(again.st_dev,again.st_ino,again.st_mode),'Process path identity changed')
    return {'target':target,'dev':s.st_dev,'ino':s.st_ino,'mode':s.st_mode}
def executable_one(pid,proc=Path('/proc')):
    """Registration identity/executable proof ONLY; no FD/opener absence claim."""
    before=identity(pid,proc);p=proc/str(pid)
    exe=os.readlink(p/'exe');exe_hash=sha_bytes((p/'exe').read_bytes())
    after=identity(pid,proc);need(stable(before,after),'Identity changed while reading executable')
    return {'identity':after,'exe':exe,'exe_sha256':exe_hash}
def scan_one(pid,proc=Path('/proc')):
    before=identity(pid,proc);p=proc/str(pid);fds=[]
    cwd=path_reference(p/'cwd');root=path_reference(p/'root')
    mount_namespace=os.readlink(p/'ns'/'mnt');mountinfo=(p/'mountinfo').read_text()
    # Keep scandir FD open until all entries are consumed, including self FD.
    with os.scandir(p/'fd') as entries:
        for entry in entries:

            flags=[int(x.split()[1],8) for x in lines if x.startswith('flags:')]
            need(len(flags)==1,'FD flags absent/ambiguous')
            fds.append({'fd':int(entry.name),'dev':s.st_dev,'ino':s.st_ino,'flags':flags[0],'socket':stat.S_ISSOCK(s.st_mode),'target':ref['target'],'mode':ref['mode']})
    after=identity(pid,proc);need(stable(before,after),'PID/credential changed during census')

    need(mount_namespace==os.readlink(p/'ns'/'mnt') and mountinfo==(p/'mountinfo').read_text(),'Mount namespace/table changed during census')
    return {'identity':after,'fds':fds,'exe':exe,'exe_sha256':exe_hash,'cwd':cwd,'root':root,'mount_namespace':mount_namespace,'mountinfo':mountinfo}
DIAGNOSTICS=[]
DIAGNOSTIC_BYTES=0
DIAGNOSTIC_SINK=None

def diagnostic(event):
    # Diagnostic-write failure cannot replace an inspection error or block cleanup.
    if len(DIAGNOSTICS)<128:DIAGNOSTICS.append(event)
    if DIAGNOSTIC_SINK:
        try:DIAGNOSTIC_SINK(event)
        except BaseException:pass

def bounded_helper(cmd,timeout=10,validate_json=False):
    global DIAGNOSTIC_BYTES
    import selectors
    p=subprocess.Popen(cmd,stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.PIPE,close_fds=True,cwd='/')
    info={'helper_pid':p.pid,'argv':cmd,'started_ns':time.monotonic_ns(),'timeout':False,'truncated':False,'read_error':None}
    buffers={'stdout':bytearray(),'stderr':bytearray()};sel=selectors.DefaultSelector();deadline=time.monotonic()+timeout
    try:
        try:info['helper_identity']=identity(p.pid)
        except BaseException as e:info['helper_identity_error']=repr(e)
        for name in buffers:
            stream=getattr(p,name);os.set_blocking(stream.fileno(),False);sel.register(stream,selectors.EVENT_READ,name)
        while sel.get_map():
            remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
            if remaining<=0:info['timeout']=True;break
            for key,_ in sel.select(min(.05,remaining)):
                chunk=os.read(key.fd,8192)
                if not chunk:sel.unregister(key.fileobj);continue
                name=key.data;room=65536-len(buffers[name])
                keep=chunk[:max(0,room)];buffers[name].extend(keep)
                if len(keep)<len(chunk):info['truncated']=True;break
            if info['truncated']:break
    except BaseException as e:info['read_error']=repr(e)
    finally:
        sel.close()
        # Exact Popen child only; never leave helper running when diagnostics fill.
        try:
            if info['timeout'] or info['truncated'] or info['read_error']:
                if p.poll() is None:p.kill()
            try:info['returncode']=p.wait(timeout=max(.01,deadline-time.monotonic()))
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                info['timeout']=True;p.kill();info['returncode']=p.wait(timeout=2)
        finally:
            p.stdout.close();p.stderr.close()
    info['finished_ns']=time.monotonic_ns()
    if validate_json and not(info['timeout'] or info['truncated'] or info['read_error']) and info['returncode']==0:
        try:json.loads(buffers['stdout'])
        except (ValueError,UnicodeError) as e:info['read_error']='Invalid complete helper JSON: '+repr(e)
    failed=bool(info['timeout'] or info['truncated'] or info['read_error'] or info['returncode']!=0)
    info['streams']={}
    for name,data in buffers.items():
        entry={'bytes':len(data),'sha256':sha_bytes(data)}
        if failed:
            retained=data[:max(0,1048576-DIAGNOSTIC_BYTES)];DIAGNOSTIC_BYTES+=len(retained)
            entry.update(retained_bytes=len(retained),retained_sha256=sha_bytes(retained),hex=retained.hex())
            if len(retained)!=len(data):info['truncated']=True
        info['streams'][name]=entry
    info['complete']=not(info['timeout'] or info['truncated'] or info['read_error'])
    diagnostic(info)
    return bytes(buffers['stdout']),info

def inspect_one(pid,proc=Path('/proc'),*,executable_only=False):
    """Complete FD+executable inspection, with bounded credential fallback everywhere."""
    with FD_LOCK:
        before=identity(pid,proc)
        try:row=executable_one(pid,proc) if executable_only else scan_one(pid,proc)
        except PermissionError:
            need(proc==Path('/proc'),'Credential fallback unavailable for synthetic proc')
            need(len(set(before['uids']))==1 and len(set(before['gids']))==1,'Mixed target credentials')
            cmd=['/usr/bin/setpriv','--no-new-privs','--reuid='+before['uids'][0],'--regid='+before['gids'][0],
                 ('--groups='+','.join(before['groups']) if before['groups'] else '--clear-groups'),sys.executable,'-I',str(Path(__file__).resolve()),('--executable-one' if executable_only else '--census-one'),str(pid)]
            # run() waits/reaps on return and kills/waits on timeout; no helper survives.
            try:raw,result=bounded_helper(cmd,validate_json=True)
            except BaseException as e:
                diagnostic({'target_before':before,'mode':'executable' if executable_only else 'full-census','helper_error':repr(e)})
                raise
            try:after=identity(pid,proc)
            except BaseException as e:after={'inspection_error':repr(e)}
            diagnostic({'target_before':before,'target_after':after,'mode':'executable' if executable_only else 'full-census','helper_result':{k:v for k,v in result.items() if k!='streams'}})
            need(result['complete'] and result['returncode']==0,'Credentialed FD census failed/incomplete: '+str(pid))
            row=json.loads(raw)
        need(stable(before,row['identity']) and stable(before,identity(pid,proc)),'Inspection helper identity mismatch')
        return row

def census(proc=Path('/proc')):
    with FD_LOCK:return _census(proc)

def _census(proc):
    result=[];initial={int(p.name)for p in proc.iterdir()if p.name.isdigit()}
    for pid in sorted(initial):
        try:result.append(inspect_one(pid,proc))
        except FileNotFoundError:
            need(not (proc/str(pid)).exists(),'Live process lost FD/identity: incomplete census')
    final={int(p.name)for p in proc.iterdir()if p.name.isdigit()}
    need(not(final-initial),'New process appeared during census; unaccounted launch')
    return result

        need(last_time is None or t>=last_time,'Arrival time regression');last_time=t
    need({x.name for x in directory.glob('*.jpg')}==seen,'Extra/unlisted JPEG')
    need(len(rows)==window['frames'] and window['save_skipped']==0,'Incomplete saved frames')
    need(window['end_ns']>=window['deadline_ns'],'Early capture termination')
    need(window['deadline_ns']-window['start_ns']==15_000_000_000,'Not full15s capture')
    # byte bound includes ALL FIFO read bytes (including pre-window) + journal bytes.
    need(journal.stat().st_size==window['journal_bytes'],'Journal byte accounting mismatch')
    need(sum(r['jpeg_bytes']for r in rows)<=window['input_bytes'],'Saved JPEG bytes exceed FIFO input')
    need(window['input_bytes']+journal.stat().st_size<512*1024*1024,'Byte bound reached')
    need(len(rows)<1000,'Frame bound reached')
    arrivals=[r['arrival_monotonic_ns'] for r in rows]
    gaps=[arrivals[0]-window['start_ns'],window['deadline_ns']-arrivals[-1]]+[b-a for a,b in zip(arrivals,arrivals[1:])]
    need(max(gaps)<=window['max_gap_ns'],'Unexplained leading/interframe/trailing timing gap')
    return {'frames':len(rows),'input_plus_journal_bytes':window['input_bytes']+journal.stat().st_size,'max_gap_ns':max(gaps),'journal_sha256':sha_bytes(journal.read_bytes()),'status':'READBACK_PASS'}

class FrameStore:
    """Synchronous complete frame saving; bounds fail, never truncate as success."""
    def __init__(self,directory,reference,max_frames=1000,max_bytes=512*1024*1024):
        self.directory=Path(directory);self.directory.mkdir(mode=0o700);self.ref=reference
        self.dirfd=os.open(self.directory,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_DIRECTORY|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_CLOEXEC)
        self.journal=open(self.directory/'frames.jsonl','xb',buffering=0)
        self.frames=0;self.input_bytes=0;self.journal_bytes=0;self.last_seq=None;self.max_frames=max_frames;self.max_bytes=max_bytes
    def account(self,n):
        need(n>=0,'Negative input accounting');self.input_bytes+=n
        need(self.input_bytes+self.journal_bytes<self.max_bytes,'BYTE_BOUND_REACHED')
    def append(self,frame,arrival_ns,read_ordinal):
        ok,seq,why=self.ref.check_contract(frame);need(ok and seq is not None and seq>0,'JPEG_CONTRACT: '+why)
        need(self.last_seq is None or seq==self.last_seq+1,'SEQUENCE_GAP_OR_REGRESSION')
        ordinal=self.frames+1;need(ordinal<self.max_frames,'FRAME_BOUND_REACHED')
        name=f'frame-{ordinal:06d}-seq{seq}.jpg'
        row={'ordinal':ordinal,'seq':seq,'arrival_monotonic_ns':arrival_ns,'read_ordinal':read_ordinal,'jpeg_bytes':len(frame),'sha256':sha_bytes(frame),'saved_frame':name}
        b=(json.dumps(row,sort_keys=True)+'\n').encode();need(self.input_bytes+self.journal_bytes+len(b)<self.max_bytes,'BYTE_BOUND_REACHED')
        fd=os.open(name,os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREAT|os.O_EXCL|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_CLOEXEC,0o600,dir_fd=self.dirfd)
        with os.fdopen(fd,'wb')as f:f.write(frame);f.flush();os.fsync(f.fileno())
        self.journal.write(b);self.journal_bytes+=len(b);self.frames=ordinal;self.last_seq=seq
    def close(self):
        if not self.journal.closed:os.fsync(self.journal.fileno());self.journal.close()
        if self.dirfd is not None:os.close(self.dirfd);self.dirfd=None

class Observer:
    def __init__(self,fifo_path,evidence_dir,approved_background,*,executable_pins,launch_floor_ticks):
        """approved_background: explicit reviewed [{'identity':..., 'reason':...}].
        Must include every initial process other than this supervisor; never auto-approved.
        """
        flag=ctypes.c_int();need(ctypes.CDLL(None,use_errno=True).prctl(37,ctypes.byref(flag),0,0,0)==0 and flag.value==1,'Supervisor subreaper not enabled')
        self.executable_pins=executable_pins;self.launch_floor_ticks=launch_floor_ticks
        self.dynamic_executables={p for p in executable_pins if Path(p).name in {'UnrealEditor-Cmd','UnrealTraceServer','zenserver'}}
        self.network_baseline=None
        self.ancestry={};self.terminal_records={};self.lifecycle_failures=[]
        global DIAGNOSTIC_SINK
        DIAGNOSTIC_SINK=lambda event:self.event("inspection-diagnostic",diagnostic=event)
        self.path=Path(fifo_path);self.out=Path(evidence_dir);self.out.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        self.background={int(x['identity']['pid']):x for x in approved_background}
        need(len(self.background)==len(approved_background),'Duplicate background approval')
        for x in approved_background:need(bool(x.get('reason')),'Background behavior rationale required')
        self.owned={os.getpid():{'identity':identity(os.getpid()),'role':'observer-supervisor'}}
        self.setup_phase='UNTOUCHED';self.target=None;self.old=None;self.warm=None;self.event_count=0;self.handoff_pass=False
    def event(self,label,**data):
        self.event_count+=1;write_new(self.out/f'{self.event_count:04d}-{label}.json',{'label':label,'monotonic_ns':time.monotonic_ns(),**data})
    def verify_exe(self,row,dynamic=False):
        exe=row['exe'];pin=self.executable_pins.get(exe)
        need(pin is not None and row['exe_sha256']==pin['sha256'],'Executable identity/hash mismatch: '+exe)
        if dynamic:need(exe in self.dynamic_executables,'Unexpected dynamic descendant executable')
    def lifecycle_failure(self,why,**data):
        self.lifecycle_failures.append({'reason':why,**data})
        try:self.event('lifecycle-failure',reason=why,**data)
        except BaseException:pass
    def record_launch(self,pid,ticks,role,transitional_executable=None):
        i=identity(pid);need(i['ticks']==ticks and i['ppid']==os.getpid(),'Direct launch identity/parent mismatch')
        if pid in self.ancestry:need(self.ancestry[pid]['identity']['ticks']==ticks,'Direct launch receipt PID reused')
        need(pid not in self.background,'Background cannot become launch')
        proof={'identity':i,'role':role,'direct_launch':True,'parent_identity':self.owned[os.getpid()]['identity']}
        if transitional_executable is not None and set(i['uids'])=={'0'}:
            need(role=='warm-reader' and transitional_executable=='/usr/bin/setpriv' and i['sid']==pid,'Unapproved root transition launch')
            parent=proof['parent_identity']
            need(all(i[k]==parent[k] for k in ('uids','gids','groups','cap_eff','cap_prm','cap_amb')) and i['no_new_privs'] in (0,1),'Unexpected root transition credentials')
            row=executable_one(pid);need(stable(i,row['identity']) and row['exe']==transitional_executable,'Root transition executable mismatch');self.verify_exe(row)
            binary=Path(transitional_executable);st=binary.stat()
            need(stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) and st.st_uid==0 and not(st.st_mode&0o022),'Transition executable not root-controlled')
            need(sha_bytes(binary.read_bytes())==row['exe_sha256'],'Transition executable bytes differ')
            proof['root_transition']={'exe':row['exe'],'exe_sha256':row['exe_sha256'],'identity':i}
        self.ancestry[pid]=proof
    def cleanup_binding(self,pid,current):
        proof=self.ancestry.get(pid) or self.owned.get(pid);need(proof is not None,'Cleanup has no launch proof')
        expected=proof['identity'];need(current['ticks']==expected['ticks'],'Cleanup PID reused')
        transition=proof.get('root_transition')
        if transition:
            self.lifecycle_failure('Unverified root-transition launch cleanup only',pid=pid)
            need(proof.get('direct_launch') and current['ppid']==os.getpid() and current['sid']==pid,'Root transition parent/session drift')
            if set(current['uids'])=={'0'}:
                bound=dict(expected,no_new_privs=current['no_new_privs'])
                need(current['no_new_privs'] in (expected['no_new_privs'],1) and stable(bound,current),'Root transition credential drift')
                if current['state']!='Z':
                    row=executable_one(pid);need(stable(current,row['identity']) and row['exe']==transition['exe'] and row['exe_sha256']==transition['exe_sha256'],'Root transition executable drift');self.verify_exe(row)
                return bound
            require_unprivileged(current)
            need(all(current[k]==expected[k] for k in ('pid','ticks','ppid','sid')),'Transition UID1001 identity drift')
            if current['state']!='Z':
                row=inspect_one(pid,executable_only=True);need(stable(current,row['identity']),'Transition cleanup inspection drift')
                if row['exe']==transition['exe']:need(row['exe_sha256']==transition['exe_sha256'],'Transition binary changed')
                else:
                    self.verify_exe(row);need(row['exe']=='/usr/bin/python3.12','Unapproved warm transition executable')
            # Cleanup-only credentials may finish dropping after the last observation.
            # This never sets owned/executable admission and remains a failed run.
            return current
        bound=expected
        if current['ppid']!=expected['ppid']:
            need(current['ppid']==os.getpid() and proof.get('parent_identity') is not None,'Cleanup adoption lacks earlier chain')
            bound=dict(expected,ppid=os.getpid())
        require_unprivileged(current);need(stable(bound,current),'Cleanup identity drift')
        return bound
    def terminal(self,pid):
        proof=self.ancestry.get(pid) or self.owned.get(pid)
        need(proof is not None,'Terminal ancestry unproven')
        expected=proof['identity'];verified=pid in self.owned and self.owned[pid].get('exe') is not None
        try:
            a=identity(pid);need(a['ticks']==expected['ticks'],'Terminal PID reused');b=identity(pid)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            need(not Path('/proc',str(pid)).exists(),'Partial terminal identity read')
            result={'pid':pid,'identity':expected,'state':'GONE','live_verified':verified}
            self.lifecycle_failure('Gone without exact child wait',pid=pid)
            self.terminal_records[pid]=result;return result
        need(a['ticks']==expected['ticks'],'Terminal PID reused')
        bound=self.cleanup_binding(pid,a)
        need(stable(bound,a) and stable(a,b) and a['state']==b['state']=='Z','Terminal identity not stable Z')
        result={'pid':pid,'identity':b,'state':'VERIFIED_TERMINAL' if verified else 'UNVERIFIED_TERMINAL_CHILD','live_verified':verified,'prior_identity':expected,'parent_identity':proof.get('parent_identity')}
        self.terminal_records[pid]=result
        if not verified:self.lifecycle_failure('Terminal before live executable verification',pid=pid)
        try:self.event('terminal',terminal=result)
        except BaseException:pass
        return result
    def reap_terminal(self,record,*,deadline=None):
        pid=record['pid'];need(record['state'] in ('VERIFIED_TERMINAL','UNVERIFIED_TERMINAL_CHILD'),'Not a terminal wait candidate')
        current=self.terminal(pid);need(current['state'] in ('VERIFIED_TERMINAL','UNVERIFIED_TERMINAL_CHILD'),'Gone is not reaped')
        need(current['identity']['ppid']==os.getpid(),'Terminal not actual waitable child')
        if deadline is not None:need(time.monotonic()<deadline,'Original cleanup deadline before wait')
        try:waited,status=os.waitpid(pid,os.WNOHANG)
        except ChildProcessError:
            self.lifecycle_failure('ECHILD is not REAPED',pid=pid);raise Block('Exact child wait returned ECHILD')
        if waited==0:
            need(status==0,'Unexpected pending wait status')
            result=dict(current,state='PENDING_WAIT',waited=waited,wait_status=status,monotonic_ns=time.monotonic_ns())
            self.terminal_records[pid]=result;self.event('terminal-pending-wait',terminal=result)
            return result
        need(waited==pid,'Wrong PID from exact child wait')
        need(not Path('/proc',str(pid)).exists(),'PID remains/reappeared after wait; no old-identity reuse')
        result=dict(current,state='REAPED',waited=waited,wait_status=status,returncode=os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status),monotonic_ns=time.monotonic_ns())
        self.terminal_records[pid]=result
        try:self.event('terminal-reaped',terminal=result)
        except BaseException:pass
        return result
    def lifecycle_cleanup_pass(self,sig,*,pids=None,deadline=None,escalation_started=None):
        outcomes=[];errors=[];pending=[]
        records=dict(self.ancestry);records.update({p:r for p,r in self.owned.items() if p!=os.getpid()})
        if pids is not None:
            need(set(pids)<=set(records),'Cleanup selection includes unknown process')
            records={p:r for p,r in records.items() if p in pids}
        for pid,proof in records.items():
            try:
                if deadline is not None:need(time.monotonic()<deadline,'Original cleanup deadline before identity')
                try:i=identity(pid)
                except FileNotFoundError:
                    need(not Path('/proc',str(pid)).exists(),'Partial identity in cleanup')
                    prior=self.terminal_records.get(pid)
                    if prior and prior['state']=='REAPED':outcomes.append(prior)
                    else:
                        outcomes.append(self.terminal(pid));raise Block('Gone without exact child wait')
                    continue
                expected=proof['identity'];need(i['ticks']==expected['ticks'],'Cleanup PID reused')
                if i['state']=='Z':
                    t=self.terminal(pid)
                    if t['identity']['ppid']==os.getpid():
                        outcome=self.reap_terminal(t,deadline=deadline);outcomes.append(outcome)
                        if outcome['state']=='PENDING_WAIT':pending.append(pid)
                    else:pending.append(pid)
                    continue
                bound=self.cleanup_binding(pid,i)
                # A recorded direct Popen identity permits failure-path termination,
                # not live executable admission. Unknown adoption is never recorded.
                need(pid in self.owned or proof.get('direct_launch'),'Unverified live descendant cannot bypass inspection')
                need(stable(i,identity(pid)),'Cleanup signal identity changed')
                if deadline is not None:need(time.monotonic()<deadline,'Original cleanup deadline before signal')
                actual_sig=sig if escalation_started is None else (signal.SIGTERM if time.monotonic()-escalation_started<2 else signal.SIGKILL)
                self.event('cleanup-signal',pid=pid,identity=i,signal=int(actual_sig),monotonic_ns=time.monotonic_ns())
                os.kill(pid,actual_sig);pending.append(pid)
            except BaseException as e:
                errors.append({'pid':pid,'error':repr(e)});self.lifecycle_failure('Cleanup identity/wait failure',pid=pid,error=repr(e))
        return {'pending':pending,'errors':errors,'outcomes':outcomes}
    def register_owned(self,pid,start_ticks,role):
        if pid not in self.ancestry:self.record_launch(pid,start_ticks,role)
        try:row=inspect_one(pid,executable_only=True)
        except BaseException as e:
            self.lifecycle_failure('Direct launch executable unverified',pid=pid,error=repr(e))
            raise
        i=row['identity'];need(i['ticks']==start_ticks,'Owned PID reused')
        need(pid not in self.background,'Background cannot silently become owned')
        need(i['ppid']==os.getpid(),'Direct launch relationship absent')
        need(set(i['uids'])=={'1001'} and set(i['gids'])=={'1001'} and i['ticks']>=self.launch_floor_ticks,'Owned direct launch eligibility mismatch')
        require_unprivileged(i);self.verify_exe(row)
        need(Path(row['exe']).name in {'UnrealEditor-Cmd','Xvfb','python3.12'},'Unexpected direct executable')
        self.owned[pid]={'identity':i,'role':role,'exe':row['exe'],'exe_sha256':row['exe_sha256'],'launch_parent':os.getpid()};self.event('owned',identity=i,role=role,exe=row['exe'],exe_sha256=row['exe_sha256'])
    def register_descendants(self):
        try:return self._register_descendants()
        except BaseException as e:
            self.lifecycle_failure('Discovery failed',error=repr(e));raise
    def _register_descendants(self):
        # Actual ancestry / verified subreaper adoption is the ownership proof.
        rows=[]
        for p in Path('/proc').iterdir():
            if p.name.isdigit():
                try:rows.append(identity(int(p.name)))
                except FileNotFoundError:need(not p.exists(),'Transient live identity')
        self.reconcile_descendants(rows)
    def reconcile_descendants(self,rows):
        known={pid for pid,x in self.owned.items() if any(i['pid']==pid and i['ticks']==x['identity']['ticks']for i in rows)}
        changed=True
        while changed:
            changed=False
            for i in rows:
                pid=i['pid']
                if pid==os.getpid() or pid in self.background:continue
                if pid in self.owned:
                    require_unprivileged(i);prev=self.owned[pid]['identity'];need(prev['ticks']==i['ticks'],'Owned PID reused')
                    if i['state']=='Z':self.terminal(pid);continue
                    if prev['ppid']!=i['ppid']:
                        need(i['ppid']==os.getpid(),'Owned process reparented outside supervisor')
                        need((self.ancestry.get(pid) or {}).get('parent_identity') is not None,'Owned adoption lacks recorded parent identity')
                        altered=dict(prev,ppid=i['ppid']);need(stable(altered,i),'Adopted identity/credentials/session drift')
                        row=inspect_one(pid,executable_only=True);self.verify_exe(row,dynamic=True)
                        self.owned[pid]['identity']=i;self.event('owned-reparent',old_parent=prev['ppid'],identity=i)
                    continue
                if i['ppid']in known:
                    need(i['ppid']!=os.getpid() or pid in self.ancestry,'First-seen adoption has no prior chain/direct launch')
                    parent=self.owned[i['ppid']]['identity']
                    need(any(stable(parent,x) for x in rows if x['pid']==i['ppid']),'Verified parent identity changed')
                    self.ancestry.setdefault(pid,{'identity':i,'role':'descendant-observation','parent_identity':parent,'direct_launch':False})
                    require_unprivileged(i)
                    if i['state']=='Z':
                        self.terminal(pid);raise Block('Unverified terminal descendant; cleanup only')
                    need(set(i['uids'])=={'1001'} and set(i['gids'])=={'1001'} and i['ticks']>=self.launch_floor_ticks,'Unknown descendant eligibility failed')
                    try:
                        row=inspect_one(pid,executable_only=True);need(stable(i,row['identity']),'Descendant identity raced');self.verify_exe(row,dynamic=True)
                    except BaseException as e:
                        self.lifecycle_failure('Descendant live verification failed',pid=pid,error=repr(e));raise
                    self.owned[pid]={'identity':i,'role':'owned-descendant','exe':row['exe'],'exe_sha256':row['exe_sha256'],'launch_parent':i['ppid']}
                    self.event('descendant-or-subreaper-adoption',identity=i,actual_parent=i['ppid'],exe=row['exe'],exe_sha256=row['exe_sha256']);known.add(pid);changed=True
    def network_snapshot(self,rows):
        # Only known transport sockets may remain attached to background services.
        background_sockets={str(f['ino']) for r in rows if r['identity']['pid']in self.background for f in r['fds']if f.get('socket')}
        owned_sockets={str(f['ino']) for r in rows if r['identity']['pid']in self.owned for f in r['fds']if f.get('socket')}
        records=set()
        for table in ('tcp','tcp6','udp','udp6'):
            for line in (Path('/proc/net')/table).read_text().splitlines()[1:]:
                fields=line.split();need(len(fields)>=10,'Malformed network inventory')
                # TCP established/listening; all UDP endpoints can carry new control traffic.
                if table.startswith('tcp') and fields[3]not in ('01','0A'):continue
                inode=fields[9];record=(table,fields[1],fields[2],fields[3],inode)
                if inode in owned_sockets and inode not in background_sockets:continue
                records.add(record)
        snapshot={'background_socket_inodes':sorted(background_sockets),'control_network_rows':sorted(records)}
        if self.network_baseline is None:self.network_baseline=snapshot;self.event('network-admission',snapshot=snapshot)
        else:need(snapshot==self.network_baseline,'Background/control connection or listener changed')
    def corroborate(self,label,expected_reader_pids=(),writer_identity=None):
        with FD_LOCK:
            self.register_descendants();rows=census()
        seen=set();readers=[];writers=[]
        for row in rows:
            i=row['identity'];pid=i['pid'];seen.add(pid)
            approved=self.owned.get(pid)or self.background.get(pid)
            need(approved is not None,'Unapproved process: '+str(pid))
            need(stable(approved['identity'],i),'Process identity/credential/parent/session drift: '+str(pid))
            self.verify_exe(row)
            if pid in self.owned and pid!=os.getpid():
                require_unprivileged(i)
                need(row['exe']==approved['exe'] and row['exe_sha256']==approved['exe_sha256'],'Owned executable drift')
            if pid in self.background:
                need(approved['identity'].get('cmdline_sha256')==i['cmdline_sha256'] and approved['identity'].get('comm')==i['comm'],'Background executable/argv identity changed')
            for fd in row['fds']:
                if self.target and (fd['dev'],fd['ino'])==self.target:
                    mode=fd['flags']&os.O_ACCMODE
                    if mode in (os.O_RDONLY,os.O_RDWR):readers.append(pid)
                    if mode in (os.O_WRONLY,os.O_RDWR):
                        need(pid in self.owned,'Unowned/background FIFO writer');writers.append(pid)
        need(set(self.background)<=seen,'Fixed background process disappeared')
        self.network_snapshot(rows)
        need(set(readers)==set(expected_reader_pids),'Unexpected/missing FIFO reader')
        if writer_identity is not None:
            now=identity(writer_identity['pid']);need(now['ticks']==writer_identity['ticks'],'Writer PID reused')
            need(set(writers)<={writer_identity['pid']},'Foreign FIFO writer')
        if self.target:self.check_inode()
        self.event(label,processes=rows,readers=readers,writers=writers)
        return rows
    def check_inode(self):
        s=self.path.lstat();need(stat.S_ISFIFO(s.st_mode) and (s.st_dev,s.st_ino)==self.target and s.st_uid==1001 and stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)==0o600,'FIFO identity/mode changed')
    def prepare_fresh(self):
        need(os.geteuid()==0,'Observer must be root')
        need(self.path.is_absolute(),'FIFO path must be absolute')
        for ancestor in self.path.parents:
            a=ancestor.lstat();need(stat.S_ISDIR(a.st_mode) and not stat.S_ISLNK(a.st_mode) and not(a.st_mode&0o022),'Unsafe FIFO ancestor')
        s=self.path.parent.lstat();need(stat.S_ISDIR(s.st_mode) and s.st_uid==1001 and stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)==0o700,'FIFO parent must be UID1001mode0700')
        if self.path.exists()or self.path.is_symlink():
            s=self.path.lstat();need(stat.S_ISFIFO(s.st_mode),'Existing IPC not plain FIFO');self.target=(s.st_dev,s.st_ino)
            rows=self.corroborate('initial-old-inode',())
            need(not any((f['dev'],f['ino'])==self.target for r in rows for f in r['fds']),'Old FIFO still open')
            old=self.out/'preserved-original-fifo';need(not old.exists() and not old.is_symlink(),'Old FIFO preservation collision')
            os.rename(self.path,old)
            self.old=old;self.setup_phase='ORIGINAL_MOVED'
        else:self.corroborate('initial-no-fifo',())
        # Install only after configuration: failures leave the canonical path absent.
        # Keep original identity separate; target refers only to an installed FIFO.
        self.preserved_target=self.target;self.target=None;self.partial_setup=True
        self.pending=self.out/'pending-fresh-fifo';self.pending_target=None
        need(not self.pending.exists() and not self.pending.is_symlink(),'Fresh FIFO staging collision')
        os.mkfifo(self.pending,0o600)
        s=self.pending.lstat();need(stat.S_ISFIFO(s.st_mode),'Staged FIFO type changed');self.pending_target=(s.st_dev,s.st_ino)
        os.chown(self.pending,1001,1001);os.chmod(self.pending,0o600)
        s=self.pending.lstat();need(stat.S_ISFIFO(s.st_mode) and (s.st_dev,s.st_ino)==self.pending_target and s.st_uid==1001 and stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)==0o600,'Staged FIFO identity/mode changed')
        need(not self.path.exists() and not self.path.is_symlink(),'Foreign canonical FIFO appeared')
        os.rename(self.pending,self.path);self.target=self.pending_target;self.partial_setup=False;self.setup_phase='FRESH_INSTALLED'
        self.check_inode();self.event('fresh-inode',dev=s.st_dev,ino=s.st_ino)
        rows=self.corroborate('fresh-no-opener',())
        need(not any((f['dev'],f['ino'])==self.target for r in rows for f in r['fds']),'Fresh FIFO already opened')
        return self.target
    def start_warm_reader(self):
        need(self.warm is None and self.target is not None,'Warm reader already exists or FIFO absent');self.check_inode()
        args=['/usr/bin/setpriv','--no-new-privs','--reuid=1001','--regid=1001','--clear-groups',sys.executable,'-I',str(Path(__file__).resolve()),'--warm-reader',str(self.path),'--dev',str(self.target[0]),'--ino',str(self.target[1])]
        p=subprocess.Popen(args,stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,start_new_session=True,close_fds=True,cwd='/')
        self.warm=p # tracked before identity lookup, caller finally must cleanup even if lookup fails
        deadline=time.monotonic()+3
        while True:
            i=identity(p.pid)
            if p.pid not in self.ancestry:self.record_launch(p.pid,i['ticks'],'warm-reader',transitional_executable='/usr/bin/setpriv')
            need(i['state']!='Z','Warm reader terminal before identity registration')
            if set(i['uids'])=={'1001'} and set(i['gids'])=={'1001'}:break
            need(time.monotonic()<deadline,'Warm reader credential transition timeout');time.sleep(.005)
        self.record_launch(p.pid,i['ticks'],'warm-reader',transitional_executable='/usr/bin/setpriv')
        self.register_owned(p.pid,i['ticks'],'warm-reader');self.event('warm-start',pid=p.pid)
        while time.monotonic()<deadline:
            need(identity(p.pid)['state']!='Z','Warm reader terminal before handoff')
            row=inspect_one(p.pid)
            if any((f['dev'],f['ino'])==self.target and f['flags']&os.O_ACCMODE==os.O_RDONLY for f in row['fds']):
                self.corroborate('warm-open',(p.pid,));return p
            time.sleep(.02)
        raise Block('Warm reader open timeout')
    def stop_warm_reader(self,writer_identity,main_path):
        need(self.warm is not None,'No owned warm reader');p=self.warm
        need(identity(p.pid)['ticks']==self.owned[p.pid]['identity']['ticks'],'Warm reader PID reused')
        before=main_status(main_path,writer_identity['pid'],self.path);need(before.get('reader')is True,'Warm mainstatus must show live reader')
        stop_utc=time.time();self.event('warm-stop-request',main=before)
        deadline=time.monotonic()+3
        need(stable(self.owned[p.pid]['identity'],identity(p.pid)),'Warm signal identity drift');os.kill(p.pid,signal.SIGTERM)
        while identity(p.pid)['state']!='Z':
            need(time.monotonic()<deadline,'Warm terminal timeout');time.sleep(.01)
        while True:
            need(time.monotonic()<deadline,'Warm exact wait exceeded original3s deadline')
            outcome=self.reap_terminal(self.terminal(p.pid),deadline=deadline)
            if outcome['state']=='REAPED':p.returncode=outcome['returncode'];break
            need(outcome['state']=='PENDING_WAIT','Warm wait not pending or reaped')
            time.sleep(min(.01,max(0,deadline-time.monotonic())))
        need(not Path('/proc',str(p.pid)).exists(),'Warm reader not fully reaped')
        # Own descendants holding inherited reader FDs are rejected, not excused.
        self.corroborate('warm-reaped-no-reader',(),writer_identity)
        while time.monotonic()<deadline:
            need(identity(writer_identity['pid'])['ticks']==writer_identity['ticks'],'Writer lost')
            m=main_status(main_path,writer_identity['pid'],self.path)
            if m.get('reader')is False and m['updated_utc']>=stop_utc and m['updated_utc']>before['updated_utc']:
                self.event('handoff-reader-false',main=m,writer=writer_identity);self.handoff_pass=True;return m
            time.sleep(.02)
        raise Block('Readerfalse/reap/census exceeded3s')
    def capture(self,writer_identity,sample_callback,start_gate,gate_timeout=6,seconds=15,max_gap_ms=250):
        need(seconds==15 and self.handoff_pass,'Capture requires successful handoff/full15s')
        need(0<max_gap_ms<=250,'Gap threshold must be predeclared<=250ms')
        self.corroborate('pre-collector',(),writer_identity);self.check_inode()
        ref=load_contract();store=FrameStore(self.out/'frames',ref);fd=None;started=None;ended=None;reads=0;precapture_frames=0;last_sample=0;last_census=0;window={};failure=None
        try:
            fd=os.open(self.path,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_NONBLOCK|os.O_CLOEXEC|os.O_NOFOLLOW)
            s=os.fstat(fd);need((s.st_dev,s.st_ino)==self.target,'Collector opened otherinode')
            self.corroborate('collector-open',(os.getpid(),),writer_identity)
            poller=select.poll();poller.register(fd,select.POLLIN);walker=ref.Walker();gate_deadline=time.monotonic_ns()+int(gate_timeout*1e9)
            while True:
                now=time.monotonic_ns()
                if started is not None and now>=started+15_000_000_000:ended=now;break
                if started is None:need(now<gate_deadline,'Pre-boundary gate timeout')
                if now-last_sample>=80_000_000:
                    if last_sample:need(now-last_sample<=250_000_000,'Receipt sample scheduling gap')
                    sample=sample_callback();sample_end=time.monotonic_ns();need(sample_end-now<=250_000_000,'Receipt callback too slow');last_sample=now
                    need(identity(writer_identity['pid'])['ticks']==writer_identity['ticks'],'Writer lost duringcapture')
                    if started is None and precapture_frames>0 and start_gate(sample):
                        # Retain parser continuity; journal timestamps identify frame completion, not SOI/exposure.
                        start_cuts,start_garbage=walker.cuts,walker.garbage
                        started=sample_end;self.event('capture-window-start',start_ns=started,deadline_ns=started+15_000_000_000,sample=sample)
                if now-last_census>=1_000_000_000:
                    self.corroborate('collector-snapshot',(os.getpid(),),writer_identity);last_census=time.monotonic_ns()
                deadline=(started+15_000_000_000)if started is not None else gate_deadline
                remaining=deadline-time.monotonic_ns()
                if remaining<=0:continue
                poller.poll(max(0,min(20,int(remaining/1_000_000))))
                if time.monotonic_ns()>=deadline:continue
                try:data=os.read(fd,1<<20)
                except BlockingIOError:continue
                arrival=time.monotonic_ns();reads+=1
                if not data:
                    if started is not None:raise Block('Writer EOF during capture')
                    time.sleep(.005);continue
                store.account(len(data))
                need(arrival<deadline,'Read completion crossed deadline; boundary ambiguous')
                whole=walker.feed(data)
                if started is None:precapture_frames+=len(whole);continue
                need(walker.cuts==start_cuts and walker.garbage==start_garbage,'JPEG cut/garbage during window')
                for frame in whole:store.append(frame,arrival,reads)
            need(started is not None,'No window')
            # trailing partial JPEG is normal for a wall-clock cut and explicitly reported.
            window={'start_ns':started,'deadline_ns':started+15_000_000_000,'end_ns':ended,'frames':store.frames,'save_skipped':0,'input_bytes':store.input_bytes,'journal_bytes':store.journal_bytes,'max_gap_ns':int(max_gap_ms*1e6),'prewindow_frames':precapture_frames,'tail_bytes':len(walker.buffer),'timing_scope':'read completion; frames from same read share timestamp','bound_scope':'allFIFOinput includingprewindow + frames.jsonl; JPEG subset not doublecounted'}
        except BaseException as e:
            failure=type(e).__name__+': '+str(e);raise
        finally:
            if fd is not None:os.close(fd)
            store.close();self.event('capture-closed',failure=failure,window=window,frames=store.frames,input_bytes=store.input_bytes)
        self.corroborate('post-collector',(),writer_identity)
        write_new(self.out/'frames'/'window.json',window)
        return window
    def validate_saved(self):
        ref=load_contract();w=json.loads((self.out/'frames'/'window.json').read_text())
        r=inspect_frames(self.out/'frames',ref,w);write_new(self.out/'readback.json',r);return r
    def restore_fifo(self):
        # Before any installation, the canonical inode is never ours to unlink.
        # Still require complete census and no opener proof for a known original.
        if getattr(self,'setup_phase','UNTOUCHED')=='UNTOUCHED' and not getattr(self,'partial_setup',False):
            rows=self.corroborate('cleanup-untouched',())
            if self.target is not None:
                need(not any((f['dev'],f['ino'])==self.target for r in rows for f in r['fds']),'Untouched original FIFO still open')
                self.check_inode()
            self.event('fifo-untouched',original_identity=self.target)
            return
        if getattr(self,'partial_setup',False):
            # Full census still applies, but no installed canonical inode exists yet.
            need(self.target is None,'Partial setup has installed target')
            rows=self.corroborate('cleanup-partial-setup',())
            need(not self.path.exists() and not self.path.is_symlink(),'Foreign canonical inode blocks restoration')
            targets=set()
            if self.old is not None:
                s=self.old.lstat();need(stat.S_ISFIFO(s.st_mode) and (s.st_dev,s.st_ino)==self.preserved_target,'Preserved original inode changed')
                targets.add(self.preserved_target)
            pending_stat=None
            try:pending_stat=self.pending.lstat()
            except FileNotFoundError:pass
            if pending_stat is not None:
                need(stat.S_ISFIFO(pending_stat.st_mode),'Staged FIFO replaced')
                observed=(pending_stat.st_dev,pending_stat.st_ino);targets.add(observed)
                if self.pending_target is not None:need(observed==self.pending_target,'Staged FIFO inode changed')
            need(not any((f['dev'],f['ino']) in targets for r in rows for f in r['fds']),'Partial setup FIFO still open')
            # If initial lstat failed, ownership of the staged inode was not recorded.
            # Preserve it for diagnosis; never delete an unproven inode.
            if pending_stat is not None and self.pending_target is not None:os.unlink(self.pending)
            if self.old is not None:os.rename(self.old,self.path)
            self.partial_setup=False;self.setup_phase='RESTORED'
            self.event('fifo-restored-partial',original_present=self.old is not None,unproven_staging_preserved=pending_stat is not None and self.pending_target is None)
            return
        need(getattr(self,'setup_phase','UNTOUCHED')=='FRESH_INSTALLED','No installed fresh FIFO to remove')
        rows=self.corroborate('cleanup-no-reader',())
        need(not any((f['dev'],f['ino'])==self.target for r in rows for f in r['fds']),'FIFO still open duringrestore')
        self.check_inode();os.unlink(self.path)
        if self.old is not None:os.rename(self.old,self.path)
        self.event('fifo-restored',original_present=self.old is not None);self.target=None;self.setup_phase='RESTORED'

def warm_reader(path,dev,ino):
    stopped=False
    def stop(*_):
        nonlocal stopped;stopped=True
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM,stop);signal.signal(signal.SIGINT,stop)
    fd=os.open(path,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_NONBLOCK|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_CLOEXEC)
    try:
        s=os.fstat(fd);need(stat.S_ISFIFO(s.st_mode)and(s.st_dev,s.st_ino)==(dev,ino),'Warmreaderinode mismatch')
        poller=select.poll();poller.register(fd,select.POLLIN)
        while not stopped:
            poller.poll(50)
            try:
                if not os.read(fd,1<<20):time.sleep(.01)
            except BlockingIOError:pass
    finally:os.close(fd)
if __name__=='__main__':
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser();ap.add_argument('--census-one',type=int);ap.add_argument('--executable-one',type=int);ap.add_argument('--warm-reader',type=Path);ap.add_argument('--dev',type=int);ap.add_argument('--ino',type=int);a=ap.parse_args()
    if a.census_one is not None:print(json.dumps(scan_one(a.census_one)))
    elif a.executable_one is not None:print(json.dumps(executable_one(a.executable_one)))
    elif a.warm_reader is not None:warm_reader(a.warm_reader,a.dev,a.ino)
    else:ap.error('No direct runtime mode; supervisor owns observer')
