#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read the game's main-view FIFO for a while and check the v24 contract (a go/no-go helper; standard library only).

    python3 tools/native_streamer_visual_v2/main_fifo_probe.py [--fifo PATH] [--seconds 30] [--contract main|any]
                                                               [--save DIR --save-count 3] [--force]

Use it ONLY while nobody else reads the FIFO: a FIFO's readers split its bytes, so a second reader breaks frames for both. It refuses
when another process holds the path open for reading (/proc scan; --force skips the check) and never creates or replaces the FIFO.
Run it as root on the pod: another user's descriptors are invisible, so it also refuses when processes cannot be checked. The
scan is advisory (a reader may open the FIFO after it): it is repeated every RESCAN_S s and at the end; `other_reader_seen` true
(with `other_readers_during_run`) means the run's numbers do not count, false means none was SEEN (not proof). Exclusivity comes
from the procedure: the director must not be reading main.mjpeg (x11 mode, or stopped), and its own FIFO tap must not be draining it.

It opens the FIFO O_RDONLY|O_NONBLOCK (no writer yet = EOF, tolerated), polls, and walks whole JPEGs by their markers (header segments
by length, entropy data to the EOI; a frame whose tail never came, or garbage, resyncs on the next SOI). `--contract main` checks every
whole frame against install_v24.py's contract: SOI, the JFIF APP0 first, ONE COM "seq=<n>" second, no other APPn/COM, SOF0 (baseline)
1920x1080, EOI. `--contract any` only counts frames (cam2). The summary (JSON): frames, contract failures, cuts, a truncated tail,
sequences (first, last, distinct, missing, out of order), frame rates (first to last frame, and `*_window` = first frame to the END
of the run, so a writer that stopped early shows; `trailing_idle_ms`), inter-frame arrival gaps (p50/p95/p99/max ms; a frame's
arrival is the read that completed it, so frames completed by one read share it: `max_frames_per_read`) and the longest silence
between reads, the trailing one included. `--save` keeps the first whole frames as .jpg files for a visual check against the windowed
game (never replacing a file, never through a link). The FIFO is opened first (O_NOFOLLOW) and checked with fstat.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import errno
import json
import os
import select
import stat
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

DEFAULT_FIFO = '/home/unreal/.sub01-private/streamer/main.mjpeg'
WIDTH, HEIGHT, COM_PREFIX = 1920, 1080, b'seq='
CHUNK = 1 << 20
RESCAN_S = 5.0   # the other-readers scan is repeated this often during a run (and once at its end)


def other_readers(path: Path, proc: Path = Path('/proc'), target: tuple | None = None) -> tuple:
    """-> (PIDs other than this one holding `path` open for reading (O_RDONLY or O_RDWR), from /proc/<pid>/fd and fdinfo;
    the number of processes whose descriptors this user may not list: another user's reader would be invisible).
    `target` = (st_dev, st_ino) of the FIFO already opened (else `path` is stat'ed)."""
    if target is None:
        info = os.stat(path)
        target = (info.st_dev, info.st_ino)
    found, unchecked = [], 0
    for entry in proc.iterdir():
        if not entry.name.isdigit() or int(entry.name) == os.getpid():
            continue
        try:
            try:
                fds = list((entry / 'fd').iterdir())
            except PermissionError:
                unchecked += 1
                continue
            for fd in fds:
                try:
                    st = os.stat(fd)
                except OSError:
                    continue
                if (st.st_dev, st.st_ino) != tuple(target):
                    continue
                flags = 0
                for line in (entry / 'fdinfo' / fd.name).read_text().splitlines():
                    if line.startswith('flags:'):
                        flags = int(line.split()[1], 8)
                if flags & os.O_ACCMODE in (os.O_RDONLY, os.O_RDWR):
                    found.append(int(entry.name))
                    break
        except OSError:
            continue
    return found, unchecked


def check_contract(frame: bytes) -> tuple:
    """-> (ok, seq or None, reason) for one JPEG; never raises. Checked: SOI ... EOI, every header segment's length within the frame
    (before the EOI), the JFIF APP0 first, ONE COM "seq=<1..19 digits>" second, no other APPn/COM, exactly one SOF0 (baseline)
    1920x1080, an SOS. The entropy data is the frame cutter's part (any marker but RST/EOI there cuts the frame)."""
    if len(frame) < 6 or frame[:2] != b'\xff\xd8' or frame[-2:] != b'\xff\xd9':
        return False, None, 'not SOI ... EOI'
    pos, segments, seq, sof, sos, end = 2, 0, None, None, False, len(frame) - 2
    while pos + 4 <= end:
        if frame[pos] != 0xFF:
            return False, seq, 'not a marker where one must be'
        marker = frame[pos + 1]
        length = (frame[pos + 2] << 8) | frame[pos + 3]
        if length < 2 or pos + 2 + length > end:
            return False, seq, 'a segment length out of bounds'
        if marker == 0xDA:
            sos = True
            break
        body = frame[pos + 4:pos + 2 + length]
        segments += 1
        if segments == 1 and marker != 0xE0:
            return False, seq, 'APP0 is not the first segment'
        if segments == 1 and not body.startswith(b'JFIF\0'):
            return False, seq, 'APP0 is not JFIF'
        if segments == 2:
            digits = body[len(COM_PREFIX):]
            if marker != 0xFE or not body.startswith(COM_PREFIX) or not 1 <= len(digits) <= 19 or not digits.isdigit():
                return False, seq, 'the second segment is not COM "seq=<n>"'
            seq = int(digits)
        elif segments > 2 and (0xE0 <= marker <= 0xEF or marker == 0xFE):
            return False, seq, 'another APPn/COM segment'
        elif marker in (0xC1, 0xC2, 0xC3, 0xC5, 0xC6, 0xC7, 0xC9, 0xCA, 0xCB, 0xCD, 0xCE, 0xCF):
            return False, seq, 'not baseline'
        elif marker == 0xC0:
            if sof is not None:
                return False, seq, 'two SOF0'
            if len(body) < 5:
                return False, seq, 'SOF0 too short'
            sof = ((body[3] << 8) | body[4], (body[1] << 8) | body[2])
        pos += 2 + length
    if not sos:
        return False, seq, 'no SOS'
    if sof != (WIDTH, HEIGHT):
        return False, seq, f'SOF0 {sof} is not {WIDTH}x{HEIGHT}'
    return True, seq, ''


class Walker:
    """Incremental frame cutter: feed bytes, get whole frames; cuts and garbage resync on the next SOI."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.buffer = bytearray()
        self.cuts = self.garbage = 0

    def drop_partial(self) -> None:
        """The writer closed (EOF): a frame it had not finished can never complete (it never resumes a tail): count it as a cut."""
        if self.buffer:
            self.cuts += 1
            self.buffer.clear()

    def _soi(self, start: int) -> int:
        return self.buffer.find(b'\xff\xd8\xff', start)

    def feed(self, data: bytes) -> list:
        self.buffer += data
        frames = []
        while True:
            start = self._soi(0)
            if start < 0:
                keep = 2 if self.buffer.endswith(b'\xff\xd8') else 1 if self.buffer.endswith(b'\xff') else 0
                if len(self.buffer) > keep:
                    self.garbage += 1
                del self.buffer[:len(self.buffer) - keep]
                return frames
            if start:
                self.garbage += 1
                del self.buffer[:start]
            end, complete = self._walk()
            fresh = self._soi(2)   # a NEW frame's SOI after this one's start (FF D8 FF never occurs inside an encoder's frame)
            if fresh > 0 and (end is None or end > fresh):
                # the writer restarted (it closed on a cut and reopened with no EOF seen in between): this frame can never complete,
                # and a walk that ran past the new SOI would glue two frames together
                self.cuts += 1
                del self.buffer[:fresh]
                continue
            if end is None:
                return frames                    # unfinished: wait for more bytes
            if not complete:                     # a cut frame: resync on the next SOI after this one
                self.cuts += 1
                nxt = self._soi(2)
                del self.buffer[:nxt if nxt > 0 else end]   # (end >= 2: from the cut on, a next SOI still arriving is kept)
                continue
            frames.append(bytes(self.buffer[:end]))
            del self.buffer[:end]

    def _walk(self) -> tuple:
        """-> (end, True) for a whole frame, (cut position, False) for a cut one, (None, False) when more bytes are needed."""
        b, n, pos = self.buffer, len(self.buffer), 2
        while True:
            if pos + 4 > n:
                return None, False
            if b[pos] != 0xFF:
                return pos, False
            marker = b[pos + 1]
            if marker == 0xD8:
                return pos, False
            length = (b[pos + 2] << 8) | b[pos + 3]
            if length < 2:
                return pos, False
            if pos + 2 + length > n:
                return None, False
            pos += 2 + length
            if marker == 0xDA:
                break
        while True:
            hit = b.find(b'\xff', pos)
            if hit < 0 or hit + 1 >= n:
                return None, False
            nxt = b[hit + 1]
            if nxt == 0x00 or 0xD0 <= nxt <= 0xD7:
                pos = hit + 2
                continue
            if nxt == 0xD9:
                return hit + 2, True
            return hit, False                    # another marker (an SOI of the next frame): this one was cut


def percentile(values: list, q: float) -> float:
    if not values:
        return 0.0
    ordered = sorted(values)
    return ordered[min(len(ordered) - 1, int(round(q * (len(ordered) - 1))))]


def open_save_dir(directory: Path) -> int:
    """-> a descriptor of the --save directory (created 0700 when absent), opened O_DIRECTORY|O_NOFOLLOW: every frame is then written
    through this held descriptor, so the path cannot be swapped for a link afterwards. Raises OSError for anything else."""
    try:
        directory.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True)
    except FileExistsError:
        pass   # an existing directory is used; anything else fails the O_DIRECTORY open below
    return os.open(directory, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)


def save_frame(dir_fd: int, name: str, frame: bytes) -> str | None:
    """Write one frame in the held directory without following a link and without replacing anything (-> name, or None if it exists)."""
    try:
        fd = os.open(name, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC, 0o644, dir_fd=dir_fd)
    except FileExistsError:
        return None
    with os.fdopen(fd, 'wb') as handle:
        handle.write(frame)
    return name


def probe(path: Path, seconds: float, contract: str, save: tuple | None, save_count: int, force: bool) -> dict:
    """`save` = (the held --save directory descriptor, its path for the report) or None."""
    try:   # open first (never through a link), then check what was opened: no window between the check and the open
        fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NONBLOCK | os.O_CLOEXEC | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
    except OSError as exc:
        if exc.errno == errno.ENOENT:
            raise
        raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} cannot be opened as a FIFO ({os.strerror(exc.errno)}; a link is never followed)')
    try:
        info = os.fstat(fd)
        if not stat.S_ISFIFO(info.st_mode):
            raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} is not a FIFO (this tool never creates or replaces it)')
        if not force:
            readers, unchecked = other_readers(path, target=(info.st_dev, info.st_ino))
            if readers:
                raise SystemExit(f'refused: other readers hold {path} (pids {readers}); a second reader would split frames (--force to skip)')
            if unchecked:
                raise SystemExit(f'refused: {unchecked} processes are not visible to this user, so another reader could be missed (run it as '
                                 'root, or --force when you know nobody else reads the FIFO)')
    except BaseException:
        os.close(fd)
        raise
    walker, frames, seqs, arrivals, failures, saved, save_skipped = Walker(), 0, [], [], {}, [], 0
    read_bytes, last_read, longest_stall, eof_reads, writer_seen, max_per_read = 0, None, 0.0, 0, False, 0
    # The scan above is advisory (a reader may still open the FIFO after it): it is repeated every RESCAN_S s and at the end, and a
    # reader seen at any time voids the run (its numbers then do not count for the go/no-go).
    target, intruders, rescan_at = (info.st_dev, info.st_ino), set(), time.monotonic() + RESCAN_S
    poller = select.poll()
    poller.register(fd, select.POLLIN)
    started = time.monotonic()
    try:
        while time.monotonic() - started < seconds:
            if not force and time.monotonic() >= rescan_at:
                intruders.update(other_readers(path, target=target)[0])
                rescan_at = time.monotonic() + RESCAN_S
            poller.poll(100)
            try:
                data = os.read(fd, CHUNK)
            except OSError as exc:
                if exc.errno in (errno.EAGAIN, errno.EWOULDBLOCK):
                    continue
                raise
            now = time.monotonic()
            if not data:                          # EOF: no writer (yet, or any more)
                eof_reads += 1
                walker.drop_partial()             # a frame cut by the writer's close: the next writer starts with a fresh SOI
                time.sleep(0.05)
                continue
            writer_seen = True
            if last_read is not None:
                longest_stall = max(longest_stall, now - last_read)
            last_read = now
            read_bytes += len(data)
            whole = walker.feed(data)
            max_per_read = max(max_per_read, len(whole))
            for frame in whole:
                frames += 1
                arrivals.append(now)
                ok, seq, why = check_contract(frame) if contract == 'main' else (True, None, '')
                if not ok:
                    failures[why] = failures.get(why, 0) + 1
                if seq is not None:
                    seqs.append(seq)
                if save is not None and len(saved) < save_count:
                    written = save_frame(save[0], f'frame-{len(saved) + save_skipped:02d}{"-seq%d" % seq if seq is not None else ""}.jpg', frame)
                    if written:
                        saved.append(f'{save[1]}/{written}')
                    else:
                        save_skipped += 1
        if not force:
            intruders.update(other_readers(path, target=target)[0])   # the last look, while this probe still holds its descriptor
    finally:
        os.close(fd)
    ended = time.monotonic()
    elapsed = (arrivals[-1] - arrivals[0]) if len(arrivals) > 1 else 0.0
    window = (ended - arrivals[0]) if arrivals else 0.0   # first frame to the END of the run: a writer that stopped early counts
    if last_read is not None:
        longest_stall = max(longest_stall, ended - last_read)   # the trailing silence too
    gaps = [(b - a) * 1000 for a, b in zip(arrivals, arrivals[1:])]
    distinct = sorted(set(seqs))
    out_of_order = sum(1 for a, b in zip(seqs, seqs[1:]) if b <= a)
    missing = (distinct[-1] - distinct[0] + 1 - len(distinct)) if distinct else 0
    return {'schema': 'sub01.main-fifo-probe.v1', 'fifo': str(path), 'contract': contract, 'seconds': seconds, 'writer_seen': writer_seen,
            'bytes': read_bytes, 'frames': frames, 'contract_failures': failures, 'cuts': walker.cuts, 'garbage_runs': walker.garbage,
            'tail_bytes': len(walker.buffer), 'fps': round((frames - 1) / elapsed, 2) if elapsed > 0 else 0.0,
            'fps_window': round((frames - 1) / window, 2) if window > 0 and frames > 1 else 0.0,
            'trailing_idle_ms': round((ended - arrivals[-1]) * 1000, 2) if arrivals else None,
            'seq': {'first': seqs[0] if seqs else None, 'last': seqs[-1] if seqs else None, 'distinct': len(distinct), 'missing': missing,
                    'out_of_order': out_of_order, 'unique_fps': round((len(distinct) - 1) / elapsed, 2) if elapsed > 0 and distinct else 0.0,
                    'unique_fps_window': round((len(distinct) - 1) / window, 2) if window > 0 and distinct else 0.0},
            # arrival = the time of the read that completed the frame: frames completed by one read share it (max_frames_per_read)
            'arrival_gap_ms': {'p50': round(percentile(gaps, .5), 2), 'p95': round(percentile(gaps, .95), 2), 'p99': round(percentile(gaps, .99), 2),
                               'max': round(max(gaps), 2) if gaps else 0.0},
            'max_frames_per_read': max_per_read, 'longest_read_stall_ms': round(longest_stall * 1000, 2), 'eof_reads': eof_reads, 'saved': saved,
            'save_skipped_existing': save_skipped,
            # the scans can show another reader, never prove there was none (one could open and close between two scans)
            'other_reader_seen': None if force else bool(intruders), 'other_readers_during_run': sorted(intruders)}


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument('--fifo', type=Path, default=Path(DEFAULT_FIFO))
    parser.add_argument('--seconds', type=float, default=30.0)
    parser.add_argument('--contract', choices=('main', 'any'), default='main')
    parser.add_argument('--save', type=Path, help='directory for the first whole frames (.jpg)')
    parser.add_argument('--save-count', type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument('--force', action='store_true', help='skip the other-reader check (only when you know nobody else reads it)')
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    save = None
    if args.save is not None:
        try:
            save = (open_save_dir(args.save), str(args.save))
        except OSError as exc:
            print(json.dumps({'schema': 'sub01.main-fifo-probe.v1', 'status': 'refused',
                              'reason': f'--save {args.save} is not a usable plain directory ({os.strerror(exc.errno) if exc.errno else exc})'}))
            return 2
    try:
        report = probe(args.fifo, args.seconds, args.contract, save, args.save_count, args.force)
    except SystemExit as stop:
        print(json.dumps({'schema': 'sub01.main-fifo-probe.v1', 'status': 'refused', 'reason': str(stop)}))
        return 2
    except FileNotFoundError:
        print(json.dumps({'schema': 'sub01.main-fifo-probe.v1', 'status': 'refused', 'reason': f'{args.fifo} does not exist'}))
        return 2
    finally:
        if save is not None:
            os.close(save[0])
    report['status'] = 'ok'
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())

"""Single root-owned traversal barrier; does not claim intrinsic read-only contents.
Runtime requires exact review and verified existing ordinary UID1001 isolation.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import ctypes,errno,fcntl,json,os,stat,subprocess,time
ANCESTOR=Path('/home/sub01-recovery-stage-20261026')
LOCK=Path('/root/sub01-visual-launch.lock')
RECOVERY=Path('/root/sub01-animation-visual-20261026-attempt06-barrier')
def need(ok,why):
 if not ok:raise RuntimeError('access barrier: '+why)
def metadata(s):return dict(dev=s.st_dev,ino=s.st_ino,uid=s.st_uid,gid=s.st_gid,mode=stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode),type=stat.S_IFMT(s.st_mode))
def within(path,root):return path==root or path.startswith(root.rstrip('/')+'/')
def unescape(s):
 for a,b in [('\\040',' '),('\\011','\t'),('\\012','\n'),('\\134','\\')]:s=s.replace(a,b)
 return s
def mounts(text):
 rows=[]
 for line in text.splitlines():
  left,right=line.split(' - ',1);fields=left.split();need(len(fields)>=6,'invalid mount row')
  rows.append(dict(device=fields[2],root=unescape(fields[3]),point=unescape(fields[4])))
 return rows
def reject_aliases(text,original,ancestor,device):
 rows=mounts(text);containing=[r for r in rows if within(original,r['point'])]
 need(containing,'original effective mount absent');base=max(containing,key=lambda r:len(r['point']))
 need(base['device']==device,'original mount device differs')
 underlying=(base['root'].rstrip('/')+'/'+original[len(base['point']):].lstrip('/')).rstrip('/') or '/'
 for row in rows:
  if row['device']!=device:continue
  root=row['root'];point=row['point']
  if within(underlying,root):alias=(point.rstrip('/')+'/'+underlying[len(root):].lstrip('/')).rstrip('/') or '/'
  elif within(root,underlying):alias=point
  else:continue
  need(within(alias,ancestor),'mount alias bypasses ancestor: '+alias)
 return dict(effective=base,underlying=underlying,mount_rows=len(rows))
def no_new_privileges():
 need(ctypes.CDLL(None,use_errno=True).prctl(38,1,0,0,0)==0,'no_new_privs failed')
PROBE=r'''
import errno,json,os,sys

a,o,c=sys.argv[1:];status={x.split(':',1)[0]:x.split(':',1)[1].split() for x in Path('/proc/self/status').read_text().splitlines() if ':' in x}

class AccessBarrier:
 def __init__(self,original,control):
  self.original=Path(original);self.control=Path(control);self.fd=None;self.lockfd=None;self.active=False;self.state='NEW';self.census=None;self.expected=None;self.event_number=0;self.record_owned=False
 def record(self,event,**data):
  self.event_number+=1;p=RECOVERY/f'{self.event_number:03d}-{event}.json'
  fd=os.open(p,os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREAT|os.O_EXCL|os.O_NOFOLLOW,0o400)
  with os.fdopen(fd,'w') as f:json.dump(dict(event=event,state=self.state,utc=time.time(),monotonic_ns=time.monotonic_ns(),**data),f,indent=2);f.write('\n');f.flush();os.fsync(f.fileno())
  directory=os.open(RECOVERY,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_DIRECTORY)
  try:os.fsync(directory)
  finally:os.close(directory)
 def acquire(self):
  need(os.geteuid()==0,'root required');self.lockfd=os.open(LOCK,os.O_RDWR|os.O_CREAT|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_CLOEXEC,0o600)
  s=os.fstat(self.lockfd);need(stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode) and s.st_uid==0 and stat.S_IMODE(s.st_mode)==0o600,'launch lock metadata')
  fcntl.flock(self.lockfd,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
  RECOVERY.mkdir(mode=0o700);self.record_owned=True;self.state='LOCKED';self.record('launch-locked',pid=os.getpid())
 def open_control(self):
  # Every ancestor directory is checked without following a symlink before opening.
  for p in [ANCESTOR,*ANCESTOR.parents]:
   s=p.lstat();need(stat.S_ISDIR(s.st_mode) and not p.is_symlink(),'unsafe ancestor path')

   if stat.S_ISREG(s.st_mode):need(s.st_nlink==1,'hard-link alias cannot be ruled out: '+str(p))

 def verify(self,mode):

  with (self.original/'Sub01HomeLab.uproject').open('rb') as f:need(bool(f.read(1)),'root original read failed')
 def audit(self,rows,quiescent):
  aliases=[]
  for row in rows:
   i=row['identity']
   if quiescent:need(not ({'1001','1002'} & set(i['uids'])),'native/import UID remains')
   aliases.append(reject_aliases(row['mountinfo'],str(self.original),str(ANCESTOR),self.device))

 def quiescence(self,label):
  for n in range(2):

 def probe(self,active):

 def install(self,census):

  self.record('pre-chmod',ancestor=str(ANCESTOR),expected=self.expected,original=self.original_identity,recovery='Only exact quiescence+FD-bound identity allows restoring0710')

 def restore(self):
  need(self.active,'barrier not active');self.quiescence('barrier-final');self.verify(0o700)
  self.record('pre-restore',actual=metadata(os.fstat(self.fd)))

  self.close()
 def blocked(self,error):
  if not self.record_owned:self.close();return
  self.state='RESTORATION_BLOCKED';self.record('blocked',error=str(error),active=self.active,ancestor=str(ANCESTOR));self.close()

def need(ok,reason):
 if not ok:raise ValueError('DDC evidence: '+reason)

def digest(raw):return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()

def stable_stat(s):return (s.st_dev,s.st_ino,s.st_size,s.st_mtime_ns,s.st_ctime_ns,s.st_uid,s.st_gid,s.st_mode,s.st_nlink)

def read_pin(path,expected,*,deadline,exact_bytes=None,root_config=False):
 path=Path(path);before=path.lstat()
 need(stat.S_ISREG(before.st_mode) and before.st_nlink==1,'nonregular/symlink/multilink '+str(path))
 if root_config:need(before.st_uid==0 and before.st_gid==0 and stat.S_IMODE(before.st_mode)==0o444,'cvars ownership/mode')
 need(before.st_size==expected['bytes'],'size mismatch '+str(path))
 fd=os.open(path,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_CLOEXEC)
 try:
  need(stable_stat(os.fstat(fd))==stable_stat(before),'opened identity mismatch')
  h=hashlib.sha256();raw=bytearray() if exact_bytes is not None else None
  while True:
   need(time.monotonic()<deadline,'pin hashing deadline')
   chunk=os.read(fd,1048576)
   if not chunk:break
   h.update(chunk)
   if raw is not None:raw.extend(chunk)
  need(stable_stat(os.fstat(fd))==stable_stat(before)==stable_stat(path.lstat()),'pin changed during read')
  need(h.hexdigest()==expected['sha256'],'hash mismatch '+str(path))
  if raw is not None:need(bytes(raw)==exact_bytes,'exact config bytes differ')
  return dict(expected,dev=before.st_dev,ino=before.st_ino)
 finally:os.close(fd)

def validate_controls(args,env):
 need(isinstance(args,list) and all(isinstance(x,str) for x in args),'argv shape')
 for arg in DDC_ARGS:need(args.count(arg)==1,'required control absent/duplicate '+arg[:50])
 for arg in args:
  low=arg.lower()
  if arg in DDC_ARGS:continue
  need(not any(x in low for x in ('ddc','cvarsini','logcmds','zen','shaderworker','shadercompileworker')),'alternative cache/worker control')
  need(not low.startswith(('-ini:','-dpcvars','-dpcvar','-envafterhere','-consolevariables')),'unreviewed config/CVar injection')
 need(isinstance(env,dict),'environment shape')
 for key,value in env.items():
  need(isinstance(key,str) and isinstance(value,str),'environment values')
  low=key.lower()
  need(not any(x in low for x in ('ddc','zen','cvars','cmdlineargs','consolevariables','logcmds','envafterhere')),'cache/CVar environment override '+key)
 return True

def validate_probe(probe,cache,pak):
 need(isinstance(probe,dict) and probe.get('schema')=='sub01.ddc-access.v1','actual access probe schema')
 need(probe.get('uid')==1001 and probe.get('gid')==1001 and probe.get('groups')==[],'actual access probe credentials')
 need(probe.get('cache')==cache,'actual cache identity differs')
 need(all(probe.get(k)is True for k in ('read_ok','write_ok','cleanup_ok')),'actual read/write/cleanup incomplete')
 need(probe.get('readback_sha256')==digest(PROBE_PAYLOAD),'actual write/readback digest')
 need(probe.get('cvars_read_sha256')==digest(CVARS_BYTES),'UID1001 cvars readback differs')
 expected={'path':str(ENGINE_PAK),'dev':pak['dev'],'ino':pak['ino'],'bytes':pak['bytes'],'read_ok':True}
 need(probe.get('engine_pak')==expected,'actual UID1001 engine pak read proof')
 return True

def messages(text):
 need(isinstance(text,str) and len(text.encode())<=32*1024*1024,'log invalid/bound')
 result=[];lines=text.split('\n')
 for index,line in enumerate(lines):
  match=re.search(r'(Log[A-Za-z0-9_]+): (.*)$',line)
  if match:
   category,body=match.groups()
   need(index<len(lines)-1 and '\x00' not in body,'categorized log line partial or contains NUL')
   body=re.sub(r'^(?:VeryVerbose|Verbose|Display|Log|Warning|Error|Fatal): ','',body)
   result.append((category,body))
 return result

def runtime_log_proof(text):
 rows=messages(text);need(rows,'empty log')

 need(command.lower().count('-logcmds=')==1 and log_switches==[LOG_VALUE],'actual reconstructed logging argument missing/doubly quoted/conflicting')

 startup='Applying CVar settings from Section [Startup] File ['+str(CVARS_PATH)+']'
 linux='Applying CVar settings from Section [Startup_Linux] File ['+str(CVARS_PATH)+']'
 start=[n for n,(c,b) in enumerate(rows) if c=='LogConfig' and b==startup]
 platform=[n for n,(c,b) in enumerate(rows) if c=='LogConfig' and b==linux]
 need(len(start)==len(platform)==1 and start[0]<platform[0]<parse_at,'custom startup sections not applied before graph')
 safe='Set CVar [[DDC.Graph:'+SAFEGRAPH+']]'
 deferred='CVar [[DDC.Graph:'+SAFEGRAPH+']] deferred - dummy variable created'
 actual=[n for n,(c,b) in enumerate(rows) if c=='LogConfig' and b in (safe,deferred) and start[0]<n<parse_at]
 need(actual,'safe actual CVar setter/deferred receipt absent')
 for n,(c,b) in enumerate(rows):
  low=b.lower()
  if c=='LogDerivedDataCache':
   need('attempting to create graph' not in low and 'opened pak cache for writing' not in low,'graph fallback/write-pak')
   if not b.startswith('Parsing cache graph '):
    need(not re.search(r'\b(?:zenlocal|zenshared|zen|cloud|http|s3)\b',low),'forbidden cache store activity')
  if 'ddc.graph' in low and n>=start[0]:
   need(c=='LogConfig' and b in (safe,deferred),'unsafe or unexplained CVar assignment/state')
   need(not ('ignored' in low and 'priority' in low),'DDC setter priority rejection')
  need(not re.search(r'(?:launching|starting|executing|createproc).*\b(?:zenserver|shadercompileworker)\b',low),'forbidden helper launch log')
  need(not (c.startswith('LogZen') and any(x in low for x in ('launch','service status','running instance','http service'))),'Zen service activity')
 pak=str(ENGINE_PAK)+': Opened pak cache for reading. (1081 MiB)'
 local='Local: Using data cache path '+str(LOCAL_CACHE)+': Writable'
 pak_at=[n for n,(c,b) in enumerate(rows) if c=='LogDerivedDataCache' and b==pak]
 local_at=[n for n,(c,b) in enumerate(rows) if c=='LogDerivedDataCache' and b==local]
 need(len(pak_at)==len(local_at)==1 and min(pak_at+local_at)>parse_at,'positive EnginePak/Local capability proof missing/repeated/early')
 optional={}
 for label,path in OPTIONAL_PAKS.items():
  expected=label+': Pak cache file '+str(path)+' not found, will not use a pak cache.'
  hits=[n for n,(c,b) in enumerate(rows) if c=='LogDerivedDataCache' and b==expected]
  need(len(hits)==1 and hits[0]>parse_at,'optional pak absence outcome missing/repeated '+label)
  optional[label]={'path':str(path),'state':'ABSENT','log_index':hits[0]}
 return {'schema':'sub01.ddc-runtime-log.v1','graph':SAFEGRAPH,'command_line_index':command_at,'reconstructed_logging_argument':'-LogCmds="'+LOG_VALUE+'"','parse_index':parse_at,'setter_indices':actual,'engine_pak_index':pak_at[0],'local_writable_query_store_index':local_at[0],'optional_paks':optional,'log_bytes':len(text.encode()),'log_sha256':digest(text.encode()),'scope':'LOG_CORROBORATION_REQUIRES_INDEPENDENT_COMPLETE_PROCESS_FD_CENSUS'}

def sha(p):

def file_rows(root):

=== AUXILIARY foreground-transport.py SHA256 73cb35fa3ace684ce21a26cb99ff74cb93c0a67532706f1814ff6816cebeef7c ===
"""Local one-shot foreground transport. No provider API, retries or background jobs.
Run only after exact combined review and verified root staging/activation.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import asyncio, hashlib, json, re, shlex, sys, time
import aiohttp
POD='505jw5ph3dt7ed'
HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
SECRET=Path.home()/'.sub01/pods.json'
RECEIPT=HERE/'foreground-transport-receipt.json'
OUTPUT=HERE/'foreground-output.txt'
LIMIT=1100
# Must equal the reviewed helper constant byte-for-byte.
SHELL_COMMAND='/usr/bin/python3 /opt/sub01/animation-visual-20261026-attempt06/tools/foreground-launch.py; result=$?; exit "$result"'
async def main():
 assert not RECEIPT.exists() and not OUTPUT.exists(), 'one-shot evidence exists'
 pod=next(x for x in json.loads(SECRET.read_text()) if x['id']==POD)
 token=pod['env']['JUPYTER_PASSWORD'];base=f'https://{POD}-8888.proxy.runpod.net'
 record={'pod':POD,'transport_sha256':hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'status':'OPENING','started_utc':time.time(),'no_retries':True}
 def save():RECEIPT.write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')
 save();name=None;finished=False
 # Close the one-shot terminal-creation HTTP connection instead of retaining an
 # idle pooled control socket that would disappear during the closed experiment.
 async with aiohttp.ClientSession(headers={'Authorization':f'token {token}'},connector=aiohttp.TCPConnector(force_close=True)) as s:
  try:
   async with s.post(base+'/api/terminals',timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=30)) as r:
    r.raise_for_status();name=(await r.json())['name']
   record.update(terminal=name,status='TERMINAL_OPEN');save()
   async with s.ws_connect(f'{base}/terminals/websocket/{name}',max_msg_size=1024*1024,timeout=30) as ws:
    await ws.send_str(json.dumps(['stdin','stty -echo; export HISTFILE=/dev/null\n']))
    await asyncio.sleep(.5)
    await ws.send_str(json.dumps(['stdin','exec /bin/bash -c '+shlex.quote(SHELL_COMMAND)+'\n']))
    record.update(status='RUNNING',launched_utc=time.time());save();deadline=time.monotonic()+LIMIT
    with OUTPUT.open('x') as out:
     while time.monotonic()<deadline:
      try:msg=await asyncio.wait_for(ws.receive(),timeout=min(5,max(.1,deadline-time.monotonic())))
      except asyncio.TimeoutError:continue
      if msg.type==aiohttp.WSMsgType.TEXT:
       data=json.loads(msg.data)
       if data[0]=='stdout':
        safe=data[1].replace(token,'[REDACTED]');out.write(safe);out.flush();print(safe,end='',flush=True)
       elif data[0]=='disconnect':finished=True;break
      elif msg.type in (aiohttp.WSMsgType.CLOSED,aiohttp.WSMsgType.CLOSE):finished=True;break
      elif msg.type==aiohttp.WSMsgType.ERROR:raise RuntimeError('foreground websocket error')
    record['status']='TERMINAL_ENDED_VERIFY_RESULT' if finished else 'TRANSPORT_TIMEOUT_VERIFY_RUNTIME'
  except BaseException as e:
   record.update(status='TRANSPORT_ERROR_VERIFY_RUNTIME',error=type(e).__name__)
   raise
  finally:
   record['ended_utc']=time.time();save()
   # Never delete/kill a possibly active terminal after an ambiguous transport failure.
   # On observed normal terminal closure it has already exited; no duplicate DELETE is needed.
if __name__=='__main__':asyncio.run(main())

"""Regress observed Linux argv reconstruction; no engine/runtime inference."""
import ast,re,unittest
from pathlib import Path
import ddc_launch_checks as d
from test_ddc_launch_checks import good_log
HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent

def native_argv():
 tree=ast.parse((HERE/'visual-supervisor.candidate.py').read_text())
 method=next(n for n in ast.walk(tree)if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef)and n.name=='execute')
 value=next(n.value for n in method.body if isinstance(n,ast.Assign)and any(isinstance(t,ast.Name)and t.id=='args'for t in n.targets))
 return eval(compile(ast.Expression(value),'<frozen native argv>','eval'),{'ENGINE':str(d.ENGINE/'Binaries/Linux/UnrealEditor-Cmd'),'PROJECT':d.PROJECT,'OUT':Path('/home/unreal/.sub01-private/animation-visual-reading-20261026-attempt06')})+list(d.DDC_ARGS)

def common_unix_command_line(argv):
 # Source UnixCommonStartup.cpp264..282: skip argv0 and quote spaced options.
 result=''
 for original in argv[1:]:
  temp=original
  if ' ' in temp:
   quote=temp.index('=')+1 if temp.startswith('-')and '='in temp else 0
   temp=temp[:quote]+'"'+temp[quote:]+'"'
  result+=' '+temp
 return result

def parse_logging_value(command):
 # Source Parse.cpp265..319: quoted empty value is a successful parse.
 marker='-LogCmds=';index=command.lower().find(marker.lower())
 if index<0:return False,''
 value=command[index+len(marker):]
 if value.startswith('"'):
  value=value[1:];end=value.find('"');return True,value if end<0 else value[:end]
 return True,re.split(r'[ \r\n\t]',value.lstrip())[0]

class Tests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_full_linux_argv_old_quotes_parse_true_empty_new_complete(self):
  args=native_argv();self.assertGreater(len(args),25)
  current=common_unix_command_line(args)
  self.assertEqual(parse_logging_value(current),(True,d.LOG_VALUE))
  self.assertEqual(current.count('-LogCmds="'+d.LOG_VALUE+'"'),1)
  old=list(args);old[-1]='-LogCmds="'+d.LOG_VALUE+'"'
  broken=common_unix_command_line(old)
  self.assertIn('-LogCmds=""'+d.LOG_VALUE+'""',broken)
  self.assertEqual(parse_logging_value(broken),(True,''))
 def test_exact_argv_pass_but_quoted_split_duplicates_fail(self):
  args=native_argv();self.assertTrue(d.validate_controls(args,{}))
  bads=[args[:-1]+['-LogCmds="'+d.LOG_VALUE+'"'],args[:-1]+args[-1].split(' '),args+[args[-1]],args+['-EnvAfterHere'],args+['-logcmds=Global Off']]
  for bad in bads:
   with self.subTest(bad=bad[-4:]):
    with self.assertRaises(ValueError):d.validate_controls(bad,{})
  for key in ['UE-CmdLineArgs','LOGCMDS','EnvAfterHere']:
   with self.assertRaises(ValueError):d.validate_controls(args,{key:'injected'})

"""Deterministic lifecycle scheduling with synthetic identities, no process launch."""
import unittest
from types import SimpleNamespace as NS
from unittest.mock import patch
from test_supervisor_lifecycle import m
class Clock:
 def __init__(self):self.t=0.;self.sleeps=[]
 def now(self):return self.t
 def sleep(self,n):assert 0<n<=.05;self.sleeps.append(n);self.t+=n
class Order(unittest.TestCase):
 def setup_case(self,settle_times=None,error_pid=None):
  clock=Clock();s=m.Supervisor({});times=settle_times or {};calls=[];done=set();entries={p:{'pid':p,'ticks':p*2}for p in (11,12,21,31)}
  children={p:NS(pid=p,returncode=None)for p in entries}
  records={p:{'identity':i,'role':('xvfb'if p==31 else 'warm-reader'if p==21 else 'native'if p==11 else 'owned-descendant')}for p,i in entries.items()}
  s.children=[(children[11],entries[11]),(children[31],entries[31])]
  class Observer:
   ancestry=records;owned={};warm=children[21];lifecycle_failures=[]
   def register_descendants(self):pass
   def lifecycle_cleanup_pass(self,sig,*,pids,deadline,escalation_started):
    calls.append((clock.t,sig,set(pids),len(s.result['failures'])))
    out={'outcomes':[],'errors':[],'pending':[]}
    for p in sorted(pids):
     if p==error_pid:out['errors'].append({'pid':p,'error':'identity drift'});continue
     if clock.t<times.get(p,0):out['pending'].append(p);out['outcomes'].append({'state':'PENDING_WAIT','pid':p,'identity':entries[p],'waited':0,'wait_status':0,'monotonic_ns':int(clock.t*1e9)});continue
     done.add(p);out['outcomes'].append({'state':'REAPED','pid':p,'identity':entries[p],'returncode':-15})
    return out
  s.observer=Observer()
  def identity(p):return None if p in done else entries[p]
  return s,clock,calls,done,children,identity
 def execute(self,case):
  s,c,calls,done,children,identity=case
  with patch.object(m.time,'monotonic',side_effect=c.now),patch.object(m.time,'sleep',side_effect=c.sleep),patch.object(m,'identity',side_effect=identity):s.settle_owned()
 def test_multiple_clients_and_early_progress(self):

  self.assertTrue(s.cleanup_proven);self.assertEqual(s.result['teardown_mode'],'ordered')
  self.assertTrue(all(t>=.2 for t,_,p,_ in calls if 21 in p or 31 in p));self.assertAlmostEqual(c.t,.2)
  first_reader=next(i for i,x in enumerate(calls)if 21 in x[2]);first_x=next(i for i,x in enumerate(calls)if 31 in x[2]);self.assertLess(first_reader,first_x)
  self.assertTrue(all(x['deadline']==7.5 for x in s.result['teardown_chronology']))
